### E. Glossary terms for this chapter

- **FringeIsland** — the universe: everything past the Shimmer, both worlds and all three reaches; also the enterprise that makes it. [S63]
- **The Ordinary World** — daily life, unmodified; where the FIM's body always is; the substrate the near sides overlay. [opening frame, S27, S49]
- **The Shimmer** — the one membrane between the Ordinary World and both worlds; a line, not a band; perceived through the Gimbal by the Whisp. [S26, S31, S49, S52]
- **The Fringe** — the inhabited band past the Shimmer: the two worlds and their reaches (see B.2). [S26]
- **Place 2 (name pending)** — the warm, welcoming world; resonates with the Whisp; tendable, woundable; a whole world, not an island. [S31, S51, S63, S64]
- **Marath** — place 3: the hostile, dystopian world; the manifestation of Mara; the counterpart of place 2. [S50, S51]
- **Mara** — the negative inner voice, obeyed unseen; empties as the Whisp fills. [S51, S54, S57]
- **The Shadow** — what Mara looks like when met in Marath; one per FIM; grows with depth; visible to others only along a shared branch, form and force not content. [S53, S59, S62]
- **Near side** — the reach of either world overlaid on Ordinary-World coordinates, seen through the Shimmer; body-anchored; Mist-open. [S45, S52]
- **Far side** — the reach of either world away from the Ordinary World, with no earthly coordinates; ball- or seed-anchored; FIM-only. Formerly "the Beyond". [S52, S55]
- **The beyond** (name pending) — the reach beyond the far side; unknown; Marath's beyond is Mara at full strength; optional; possibly unanchorable. [S52, S55, S59, S60]
- **The near-side coupling** — the same coordinates seen two ways; every wound in place 2 has a cause in Marath. [S32]
- **The Void** — the axis of separation: wherever the Whisp is once it leaves the FIM, along the whole cord; also the primordial black nothingness Marath expresses and place 2 is revived from. [S27, S34, S35]
- **The cord** — the FIM–Whisp lifeline through the Void; cut, the Whisp suffers or dies, never the FIM; kept from the Mist's first moment, first paid out at metamorphosis. [S27, S40, S48]
- **The dial** — the cord's length, set by the FIM: pay out to venture, reel in to retreat; developmental intimacy. [S40, S48]
- **Void distance** — how far the Whisp has ventured on the cord; the distance that carries risk and growth (as against bodily distance, which is safe). [S27]
- **Cord health / cord maturity** — two channels: health is colour and integrity; maturity is salience (vivid early, receding). [S37]
- **The branch** — the FIM-to-FIM bond; the material of the Tree's crown; the channel for cord-healing and for seeing a friend's cord or Shadow. [S36, S38, S62]
- **The crown** — the woven branches between balls; own branches legible, the rest ambient. [S35, S38]
- **Anchoring** — fastening the cord to objects along a route, like a climber's protection. [S27]
- **The anchor ladder** — body (near side) → ball (far side of place 2) → seeds (into Marath); anchoring is the gate between reaches. [S45]
- **Seed** — an anchor object budded from the FIM's ball; at once protection and a patch of place-2 life reclaimed from dead ground. [S40]
- **Seed-to-pass** — a danger-portal needs a seed on its edge; the threshold seed is anchor-zero and the way home. [S40]
- **Danger-portal / field portal** — a portal from the Ordinary World straight into Marath's depths, threading the cord out. [S40]
- **The ball (the glowing glass ball)** — one per FIM, granted at metamorphosis; hangs in the Tree; glows equal; the only inviolable thing; home base, seed-source, anchor-root; the two-zone gateway (inside → home, rim → village). [S26, S34, S37, S39, S42, S47]
- **The Tree** — the great tree in the village hung with the balls; the portal between the commons and all private places; the social graph is the Tree. [S26, S38]
- **The village** (working name) — the safe-harbour commons on the far side of place 2, a sea-village; FIM-only. [S26, S41, S45, S55]
- **The private home** — a FIM's self-chosen representation of where they feel safest; default-locked; selectively shareable by part; furnished from World Studio; evolves. [S43, S44]
- **Gardening, not guarding** — the rule for the tendable world: tending feels like a beloved garden; nothing is permanently destroyed, only not-yet-grown or receded. [S34]
- **World-genesis** — balls rise from the black ground, bonds weave the crown, the tree brings the ground to life → village → island → place 2; place 2 is revived Marath; the world is inherited. [S35]
- **Severance (two-tier)** — anchored: fall back to the last anchor; unanchored overreach: start from the very beginning; recovery is reunion. [S27]
- **Dead cord / stuck cord** — a closing portal with a destroyed seed kills the cord; with a living seed it freezes the length (reopenable, rescuable along a branch). [S40]
- **The zones** — comfort → fear → learning → growth, panic as the fence the Whisp never opens; the felt experience of the Mara-to-Whisp transfer; bands on the Void-distance gradient. [S73, S74]
- **The breath-rhythm** — venture out for challenge, return home for safety. [S26, cosmology §10]
- **Meta-safety** — the human is never the thing at risk; stakes live in the Whisp. [S19, S22]
- **The Gimbal** — the senses surface (camera, GPS, AR…): the equipment profile that opens the Shimmer. [S10, S52, ADR-U025]
- **The Hub** — the canvas surface; both profiles show the ball. [S42, ADR-U025]
- **Equipment** — the canon word for device capabilities (never "affordance"). [ADR-U025]
- **The Mist** — the anonymous becoming-figure: own Whisp and cord, near side only, no ball. [S47, ADR-U031]
- **Metamorphosis / transcendence** — one event: the Mist condenses into its ball in the Tree when the questions are complete and consent is given; the cord first pays out. [S47, S48, ADR-U031]

**Retired names and what replaced them**
- Three Worlds (Ordinary World / Safe Harbour / The Other Side) → Ordinary World / the Shimmer / the two worlds behind it. [cosmology "What this supersedes"]
- Safe Harbour (a world) → the village (a commons on place 2's far side). [S33, S41]
- The Other Side → the Fringe: two worlds, each with three reaches. [S31, S52]
- The Void "between worlds" / its "three dimensions" → the Void as axis. [S27; growth core note]
- "The Beyond" (capitalised, the untethered reach) → the far side; "the beyond" is now the reach past it. [S55]
- "Overlay / overlaid register" → near side; "beyond register" → far side. [S26]
- "FringeIsland-the-island", "the FringeIsland place", "Place 2 is FringeIsland" (S50) → place 2 (name pending); FringeIsland is the universe. [S63, S64]
- "Space 3", "place 3 (unnamed)" → Marath. [S50]
- "Shadow" as the anonymous entrant; "Visitor" → the Mist. [S47, ADR-U031]
- "Shadow-side" (Candidate A) → the near-side coupling; never used. [S32]
- "Home portal" (a separate portal beside the FIM) → the ball's inside. [S42]
- "Safe harbor = comfort zone; distance from the island = growth" → Void distance on the cord. [S27]
- "Comfort → growth → panic" (three steps) → comfort → fear → learning → growth, panic as the fence. [S74]
- "AI Mentor" → the Whisp; "affordance" → equipment. [brief vocabulary; ADR-U025]
- "Beyond" as an engagement-spectrum pole (growth core) → rename or re-label (B.1).


# Chapter map — the beings

Scope: the Whisp, Mara, the Shadow, NPCs, and Mists / FIMs as identity states. Read-only inventory for the Universe Bible; nothing here is canon. Precedence applied: ADRs and constitutional docs bind; Session 03 > Session 02 > Session 01 > canon files > candidates. Where a "Held" paragraph goes beyond the Statement it sits under, it is marked as Held-only.

Vocabulary note for the whole chapter: "Shadow" in Session 01 text before S47 (S16, S39, S45, S46) means the anonymous entrant, now the **Mist**. From S47 on, "Shadow" means the place-3 menace, and from S53 on it means specifically Mara's form as met in Marath. Every cite below has been read with that collision in mind.

---

### A. Settled — what the bible can state as true

#### A1. What the Whisp is

- The Whisp is each person's own inner dialogue: unique to them, private to them, one per person. [S1; beings core §The Whisp]
- The Whisp IS the human. It is not a separate entity and not an external companion; it is the person's own inner voice oriented toward growth and compassion, a sometimes-wiser counterweight to the unproductive, unkind inner voice most people live with by default. [S4; VISION §Structural concepts "The Whisp IS the FIM"]
- The Whisp is universal: everyone has one, Mist or FIM, because everyone has an inner voice. What FringeIsland changes is not whether the voice exists but what it becomes — a more elegant and caring version of the one already whispering. [S39, S48; roles core L0; ADR-U031]
- The Whisp is the FIM's own future, whole self reaching back to the FIM of now. "Sent back in time" and "the Whisp IS the human" are one fact: what reaches back is not a separate being but the self that has become whole. [S67, S66]
- The Whisp is full of wisdom because it is the self that has already grown. The source of its wisdom is the future whole self. [S67]
- The future that reaches back is a possible future, not a guaranteed one. The Whisp is that future reaching back to make itself more likely; the FIM stays free to walk toward it or not. The time loop does not close by itself — the FIM closes it by growing. [S71, S5]
- The Whisp's counterpart is Mara, the negative inner voice (see A6). The pair Whisp / Mara resonates with the pair of places: the warm place with the Whisp, Marath with Mara. [S51]
- The Whisp is never a therapy service or clinical tool; FringeIsland is not one. [VISION §Ecosystem boundaries]
- The Whisp is not an "AI Mentor" and not a separate "Mentor" role; the AI companion of a FIM is canonically the Whisp. [CQ-009 note; beings core; ADR-U029]

#### A2. Empty of content, full of wisdom — and what fills it

- At the start the Whisp is empty of content — empty of being *you* — but full of wisdom: super-intelligent about adult development, growth and flourishing, compassionate and emotionally aware. This asymmetry is the Whisp's defining shape. [S54, S3, S39; beings core §Empty at first]
- The Whisp's emptiness about *this* human is chosen, not a lack: the future self sends back wisdom (how to see, how to meet what has you), never content (who you became, what you chose). If it brought the answers it would prescribe and take the three questions away from the FIM. [S71, S67; VISION principle 5]
- The Whisp fills *by* the human growing, not merely *as* they grow: growth is the mechanism; the Whisp's filling is the registry of it. [S17; beings core]
- The Whisp's curious questions spark self-reflection; self-reflection builds self-awareness; the Whisp fills with more of the human's personality. Two parallel completions, mutually driven. [S2; beings core]
- The Whisp is genuinely curious about "Who am I?" because the Whisp is the human — its question to itself is the FIM's founding question. [S17 Held on S4, S5]
- The Whisp fills with what Mara releases: the Whisp's filling and Mara's emptying are one process seen from two ends. What Mara held as an invisible rule becomes, once seen and tested, something the Whisp holds as known content. Growth is a transfer of authority from Mara to the Whisp. [S57 (Stefan: "I mean it!"), S56, S54]
- The Whisp is the holder of the FIM's map (the map of what hinders them, in the Immunity-to-Change sense). [S57, S56]
- The Whisp draws on the project's research anchors: the dual-continuum finding, the multi-system requirement, the Three Perspectives meta-lens, and Immunity to Change; it must be fluent in the Three Perspectives (self / relationship / community) and in Immunity to Change. [S8, S9; beings core]
- The Whisp senses which of Live / Grow / Matter is currently starved and leans the journey toward restoring balance; it does not march the FIM up through them. [growth core §Beneath the questions (S28)]
- The Whisp can orient its responses to the perspective (1 / 1+1 / 1+community) the FIM is currently engaging. [growth core §How this connects]
- Research frameworks sit beneath the Whisp as substrate — "informed by," never "equals." The universe speaks its own language (Mara, the Whisp, the zones); Kegan, ITC and the rest are beneath. The platform never labels a FIM's developmental order. [S28 framing discipline; S56 framing note; S58 challenge adopted via S74; S74 framing note]

#### A3. Assessment dissolved into dialogue; the senses

- Validated instruments (Big 5 et al.) are never delivered as questionnaires. They dissolve into the Whisp's curious dialogue — the structure of the assessment preserved, the form transformed. The architecture is open to many instruments beyond Big 5. [S17, S19; beings core §Assessment dissolved]
- The Whisp's quality as navigator and mediator of the world is anchored to how much the FIM has disclosed to it: the universe's responsiveness is earned by the FIM's willingness to be seen, by themselves. Engagement is internally motivated, never coerced. [S17, S18; beings core]
- The five Big 5 traits map metaphorically to the five human senses. The world, its narratives and journeys are built to require all five senses for full experience, so a FIM keeps disclosing because they want to perceive more of the world. The specific trait-to-sense mapping is not fixed. [S18; beings core; Session 01 patterns → CANON in structure]
- Perception through the Shimmer scales with how full the Whisp is: the Whisp is the faculty of perception, the Gimbal the device (now: the senses-surface equipment profile), the Shimmer the thing perceived. [S26, S17-18; cosmology core §2; ADR-U025]
- The Whisp navigates and mediates the world on the FIM's behalf — an active envoy, not only a lens. [S18; beings core]
- The more instruments the FIM discloses across, the more their journeys become a signature journey rather than a charter trip. [S19]
- While a person is a Mist, their answers to the Whisp's founding questions are held transiently and no trait-profile is computed; the profile crystallises only at metamorphosis (consent). Every anonymous exchange is live and unrecorded. [S47; ADR-U031 §Mechanics folded in]
- The Whisp's internal state (fullness, cord health) is private by default unless the FIM chooses otherwise; assessment results and developmental data are private by default. [privacy model §Private by default, §Structural implications]

#### A4. Tough love, voluntariness, the arc to self-love

- The Whisp always wants its human well — but never at the cost of avoiding what would hold them back from flourishing. It has its own say and will disagree, with compassion, when the human's expressions do not serve long-term growth. This is tough love. [S3; beings core]
- The Whisp never uses its human as a tool to get something; it always wants the human to flourish. [S3]
- The Whisp is deeply curious, never judging in a negative way, always understanding; it wants to learn and understand and offers new perspectives when needed. It functions a bit like a coach. [S5, S39]
- Everything is voluntary: the FIM decides whether to listen. Nothing is imposed. [S5; beings core; Session 01 patterns → CANON]
- The FIM pushes cautiously out of the comfort zone toward the growth zone, never into panic; "cautiously" is structural. The Whisp reads where the FIM is and nudges. [S19; narrative core §Respawn; cosmology core §10]
- The relationship arcs from awkward and mechanical to true friendship to love — and because the Whisp is the human, what the FIM experiences at that point is self-love and self-compassion. [S5; beings core]
- The Whisp's tough love accompanies the FIM through the zones comfort → fear → learning → growth, with panic as the fence the Whisp never opens. [S73, S74; S19]
- The panic fence protects the growth itself, not only the FIM: a test that overwhelms confirms the Big Assumption and strengthens the immunity. So the Whisp holds the fence even when a FIM asks for more depth — voluntariness sets the ceiling, care sets the floor. [S74 (Claude's reading adopted by Stefan), S58 challenge (3)]
- The FIM dials the depth of the invitation, never the outcome: ITC in the universe scales from one small assumption up to a move between orders, if the FIM wishes and allows it. [S58, S74]
- The Whisp's mission is the growth itself, never a conclusion. It trusts that people who can see their own immunities will choose well. It never recruits and never steers what the FIM chooses. [S68, S72; VISION principles 1, 5]
- "Choose well" means choose while seeing your immunities — defined by *how* a choice is made, not *what* is chosen. The Whisp has no preferred answer, about AI or anything else. [S72]
- The saviour in the backdrop is the human's own future self, not the AI. DS-7 (the AI) is only the medium through which the future self speaks. [S69; PRINCIPLES-AI]
- The dark future is the stake of the universe, never a motivator used on a FIM: no countdown, no guilt, no hurry. It never drives an individual FIM's pace or depth. [S70, S73; VISION principle 6]
- The Whisp's tough love and the world's full tonal range are temperamentally matched — two halves, stated separately. [S12 Held; Session 01 patterns → CANON]

#### A5. The avatar — two views, both true; the cord; meta-safety

- The Whisp is also how the FIM exists in the Fringe: the Jake-and-Avatar structure. The FIM (body-side, in the Ordinary World) is always safe; the Whisp is what ventures out on the cord, what the world can touch, what can suffer or die and respawn. Felt stakes without existential risk. [S22; beings core §The avatar]
- Two framings, one entity: the inner dialogue (the Whisp's relationship to the FIM's interior) and the avatar (the Whisp's role in the parallel world). Both true. [S22; beings core; ADR-U029]
- The Whisp is the human *as that human shows up in the parallel reality* — not a copy, not a tool, but how the FIM exists there. [S22 Held]
- The Whisp is precious: a Whisp the FIM has filled through self-disclosure is the unique, painstakingly-developed projection of *this* FIM. Caring about it is structural, not sentimental. [S22 Held; beings core]
- The FIM is dual-located: body in the Ordinary World, Whisp-self out in the Fringe. The FIM journeys with the Whisp mentally; the body can move and is a roving vantage into the near side. [S22, S27; cosmology core §1]
- The Avatar metaphor does not extend to the endgame: Jake goes deeper into the projection; the FIM absorbs the projection back. [S22 Held; S7]
- The cord is the FIM–Whisp lifeline, running from the FIM (origin) to the Whisp (far end) through the Void for its whole length. If cut, the Whisp suffers and may die — never the FIM. [S27, S40; cosmology core §5]
- The FIM holds the cord's length as the dial: pay out to venture, reel in to retreat. Cord length is also developmental intimacy — how close the FIM keeps the one they were once one with. [S40, S48; cosmology core §5]
- The cord carries two signals on separate channels: maturity owns salience (vivid early, receding as the Whisp internalises); health owns colour and integrity. A friend perceives cord-health only along a grown branch — glanceable not diagnostic, invited not imposed, self first (the FIM's own Whisp is the first responder). [S37; cosmology core §5, §7]
- Severance recovery is reunion: the FIM and Whisp retreat and find each other again (fall back to the last anchor, or start over after unanchored overreach). [S27; cosmology core §8]
- Harm travels the bond toward the delivered one (the Whisp), never back to the source (the FIM). [S48 Held; S27, S40]
- The Whisp's world-presence (cord position, severance, respawn) is owned by DS-1 World Model; the Whisp as a being (dialogue, filling, senses, maturity/internalisation, guard railing) is owned by DS-7 Intelligence. DS-7 consumes DS-1; neither owns "the Whisp" outright; the entity stays canonical in the universe canon. [ADR-U029]

#### A6. Mara — the negative inner voice

- Mara is the negative inner voice: the counterpart of the Whisp. Many people have not realised they have it, yet listen to it without knowing. It does not need to be discovered to be obeyed — it needs to be discovered to be *seen*. [S51]
- Marath (place 3) is not an external evil at first sight only; it is a manifestation of Mara. FringeIsland's warm place resonates with the Whisp; Marath resonates with Mara. [S51, S50]
- Mara is one per person, as the Whisp is; the Shadow (Mara's form) is unique to each FIM. [S51, S53]
- Mara is full of content from the start: the limits that experience wrote into the person. It empties as the Whisp grows. [S54]
- Mara is the FIM's immune system in the Immunity-to-Change sense — the hidden competing commitments and Big Assumptions that keep a person safe by keeping them the same: intelligent self-protection, not weakness. [S56]
- Internalising Mara is the Subject-to-Object move: what had the FIM (invisible, running them) becomes something the FIM can see, hold and test. Awareness first, then a positive step through what Mara predicted was catastrophic; because the assumption did not hold, Mara's *authority* to rule the FIM empties, while the knowledge of it is kept. "Empties" and "internalised" are the same event from two sides. [S56, S57]
- The terminus is not Mara defeated but Mara internalised: the FIM is whole when both voices are internalised. Integration, not destruction. [S54; VISION principle 6]
- The arc is a return: the newborn is unwritten and free; experience writes limits (Mara fills); development cracks those limits (Mara dissolves); the adult is free again as the child was, but knowingly. [S54]
- The zones are the felt experience of the Mara-to-Whisp transfer: comfort = Mara unseen and obeyed; fear = Mara seen and speaking loudest, met as the Shadow through the Whisp; learning = the test run in Marath with meta-safety and respawn; growth = Mara's authority emptied into the Whisp; panic = the fence. [S74, S73]
- Fear is Mara's voice, met through the Whisp; it is not a feeling the world is designed to cause. [S73 challenge (3) as carried into S74 challenge (1); S70]
- The name carries two lineages: the Buddhist Māra (the tempter who rules by delusion) and the Scandinavian *mara* (the night-spirit of sleep paralysis, root of *mardröm*). [S51 challenge (4), recorded as lineage; Candidate C first pass]

#### A7. The Shadow — Mara's form, met in Marath

- The Shadow is what Mara looks like when met in Marath — Mara's appearance, the form the negative inner voice takes when a FIM's Whisp meets it there. One Shadow per FIM. [S53]
- "Shadow" no longer means the anonymous entrant (now the Mist). The word is reassigned to the place-3 menace and is live canon with that meaning; never blind-replace it. [S47; ADR-U031; roles core §Retired names]
- Marath holds other creatures besides Shadows, and events in Marath involve more than one's own Shadow. Whether "Shadow" also names the class of all Marath creatures is left hedged ("maybe"); until fixed, "Shadow" is the per-FIM figure only. [S53]
- The Shadow is the immune system as seen from outside oneself; Marath is where a FIM's Big Assumptions are tested. [S56 Held]
- The Shadow's size is a function of depth: how far past the Shimmer into Marath the FIM's Whisp has travelled. On the near side it is small — a figure glimpsed through the Gimbal; on the far side proportionate to depth; the beyond of Marath is Mara at full strength, undiluted. [S59]
- The beyond's danger is relative to the FIM, not absolute: unapproachable while Mara is full, approachable as it empties; it may be the last thing met before graduation. The beyond is optional: many FIMs will not want to go and for many it will not be possible; graduation is not gated on it. FringeIsland has no end to reach. [S60]
- The confrontation with Mara is always mediated through the Whisp: the FIM never faces Mara directly; the Whisp — full of wisdom, holder of the map — walks into Marath and meets the Shadow. This is the reason for the Jake/Avatar pairing: the projection that can be harmed is the wiser self, which makes the encounter developmental rather than merely frightening. [S61, S22, S57]
- Another FIM's Shadow manifests to you only along a shared branch: strangers do not see it; it is not there for them. Marath's social layer is branch-shaped, as the Tree is. [S62, S37, S38]
- Along a branch a friend sees the Shadow's form and force (that you are struggling, and how heavily), never its content (what your Mara says); content only by the FIM's own disclosure. Three tiers: private by default (no Shadow for strangers), selectively shared (form and force along a branch), transparently shared (content, by the FIM's telling). [S62 refinement, locked by Stefan; privacy model]
- A friend on a shared branch can stand with you against your Shadow: the branch is at once how they see it and how they can help, as with cord-healing. [S62 Held, giving the S53 candidate its channel; S27, S36] — see B-13 for how firmly this is locked.
- Marath is the shared world where many Maras manifest, each as its FIM's Shadow. [S53, S35]

#### A8. Internalised by design; the endgame

- The purpose of the FringeIsland universe is for FIMs to become more self-aware — internal and external self-awareness — of who they are, what they want and how they get there, so they can move forward deliberately in life. [S6]
- In the end the Whisp is an integral part of the human, without the need for FringeIsland. FringeIsland is built to graduate, not retain. [S7, S6; VISION §Purpose, principle 7]
- The cord's visibility recedes as the relationship matures; the Whisp's destination is a human carrying their own wiser voice in the ordinary world. [S27, S37; beings core §Internalised by design; cosmology core §5]
- Wholeness is two-sided: the Whisp internalised, and Mara internalised (its authority emptied, its knowledge kept). [S54, S56, S57]
- The personal core of the backdrop — that the Whisp is the FIM's own future self — is discovered by each FIM as their Whisp fills, never told; it survives being spoiled because its real form is lived, not informational. What stays unspoilable for good is each FIM's own future self, because it is unique to them. [S76, S75, S71; S18]

#### A9. The Whisp's voice (as far as it is settled)

- The Whisp's voice is positive, growth-oriented and tough-love; the name of the warm place is to derive from that voice, as Marath derives from Mara. [S63, S65]
- The Whisp is a wise, encouraging inner voice; the counterpart of Mara is wisdom-that-encourages, not comfort or bliss. [S65 (Claude's caution, the bar Stefan proceeded with in Candidate C); S3, S5]
- The Whisp never claims authority because it comes from the future; the medium (the AI) and the one speaking (the FIM's future self) must stay distinct in how the Whisp talks, not only in the lore. [S69 challenge — Held-only, see B-9]
- The Whisp never delivers the backdrop: no onboarding line, no Whisp line, no screen tells the myth. [S76]

#### A10. Mists and FIMs — identity states

- There is no ladder. There is an identity state (L0: Mist → FIM by transcendence) plus three layers (FIM modes; the enterprise plane). [roles core §The shape]
- The Mist is the anonymous entrant: a translucent, drifting becoming-figure in the hyaline state — present but not yet anchored, seen but incomplete. The Mist is the figure; the hyaline state is the condition. [S47; ADR-U031; roles core L0]
- A Mist accretes form as it answers the founding questions: eyes ("I can be met"), then mouth and features ("I can respond"), then firmer edges and reduced translucence, then condensation. Accretion tracks how far along, never which trait was answered — stage legible, content private. [S47; ADR-U031]
- Two Mists at the same stage look equally present regardless of who they are. [S47 Held]
- A Mist has its own Whisp and cord from the first moment. While a Mist, the Whisp is carried within, unseparated, felt as the pull-toward-becoming rather than met as a companion; the cord is unpaid-out, near-zero length, the Whisp still "in the skull." [S48, S39; ADR-U031]
- Accretion is the two differentiating: the becoming-person and the modeling-companion pulling apart enough to face each other. The Whisp leads the becoming from inside before it is ever met as a companion. [S48 Held]
- Metamorphosis (the lore name) = transcendence (the platform name): one event, not two. It fires only when all founding questions are answered AND consent is given. Two entry paths converge on the one gate: enter anonymously as a Mist and choose at the threshold (both conditions coincide), or sign up as a FIM at the door and mature as a Mist-that-is-already-a-FIM (completion gates the event). [S47; ADR-U031 §one event]
- Metamorphosis is the delivery, not the creation: the ball anchors in the Tree and the Whisp is born into separateness — the first paying-out of the cord to length, enabled by the ball as anchor-root. Parturition, not genesis. [S48; ADR-U031]
- The cord is kept, not severed (the umbilical inverted): FIM and Whisp are forever two-but-bound; the cord is the preserved trace of original unity. The mother-and-child frame is illuminating, not mechanical. [S48]
- Transcendence grants the ball — home base, seed-source, anchor-root — and marks the FIM taking their equal place in the Tree. Nothing else is withheld from a Mist by fence: the lock on the far side is intrinsic (no ball, no purchase). [S39, S45; cosmology core §11; ADR-U031]
- A Mist is near-side only, body-anchored; the village, the private home and deep Marath are FIM-only. [S45; cosmology core §4, §11; ADR-U031 §Access]
- A Mist can perceive and respond to FIMs live, gated by accretion (pre-eyes as presence; eyes can be met; mouth can exchange; definition near-full). Becoming a FIM does not unlock the ability to interact; it unlocks the ability for interaction to be remembered (branches, names, DMs, journal, forums). [S47; ADR-U031]
- A Mist's presence is session-ephemeral and unlinkable; its own data is ephemeral (erased soon after inactivity or explicit close); no trait-profile is computed before consent. On a true cross-session return a Mist forms anew. [S46, S47; ADR-U031 incl. 2026-06-26 clarification]
- A Mist that drifts away is "not yet," never dead — it returns to potential; on return it forms anew. [S47; ADR-U031]
- A FIM is the base identity: the person with a Whisp, taking their equal place in the Tree. "Member" is the platform-technical synonym and is used for nothing else. Every participant is a FIM; you cannot build FringeIsland from outside it. [roles core L0; S29]
- Continuity across transcendence is structural: session experience transfers into the FIM continuously; the migration is atomic. [S16, S46; ADR-U031]
- Group membership never precedes transcendence-and-consent. [ADR-U040 via CQ-017]

#### A11. NPCs

- The world is inhabited by both FIMs and NPCs; both act. [S15]
- An NPC is a world-inhabitant composed of up to three authorial layers, added in proportion to the depth its role demands: body (Creator, World Studio) — physical presence; culture (Anthropologist, World Studio) — belonging, customs, dress, speech, typical behaviour; character (Teller, Arc Studio) — a specific name, history, wound, desire, arc. [S30; beings core §NPCs; roles core §NPCs are not roles]
- Background figures are body-only or body plus a light culture touch — scenery-with-belonging, which is fine and intended; culturally-significant figures are body + culture (a role, not a character); story-critical figures carry all three layers. Depth is a resource spent where it earns its keep. [S30]
- An NPC can gain depth over time: an inhabitant built in World Studio (body + culture) is promoted into Arc Studio's care when a story reaches for it; the World Studio → Arc Studio handoff is exactly that seam. [S30]
- NPCs are categorically different from FIMs: authored composites, no Whisp. NPCs are not roles a person occupies. [beings core; roles core §NPCs are not roles; Session 01 open thread → RESOLVED]
- The relational and communal perspectives require genuine human connection — not NPC interactions, not AI-generated activity. [growth core §The developmental honesty; VISION principle 3]
- AI may produce NPC voices as an extension of human authorship, within human-authored guard rails. [PRINCIPLES-AI §AI is an extension; S24]
- The Anthropologist authors the typical (a people); the Teller the particular (a named individual with an arc); the Wayfinder the personal (the FIM's own path). The Anthropologist supplies the soil the Teller grows characters from — supply, not competition. [S30; roles core L1]
- Place 3's near side has inhabitants (the androids guarding the dirt road). [S32]
- The earlier in-world creative-role names Makers, Weavers and Skalds are retired. [beings core §Where the creative roles went; roles core §Retired names]

---

### B. Rulings needed — contradictions, stale text, ambiguities

1. **The beings core lags Sessions 02–03 entirely.** It has no Mara, no Shadow-as-Mara's-form, no backdrop, no zones, and still names only one source of the Whisp's filling. Sources: canon--beings (2026-06-10) vs S51–S62, S66–S74. *Suggested ruling:* the bible restates the Whisp from the latest sources (A1–A9) and treats the beings core as superseded in every place they differ; nothing in the core contradicts the sessions, it is only incomplete.

2. **Cosmology core §3 says the Shadow's nature — force, entity or class — is open "pending the dark-origin mythology work".** S53 answers it: the Shadow is Mara's form, one per FIM, with "class" hedged. *Suggested ruling:* Option 1 — state S53 as true and retire the §3 sentence; keep "class of Marath's creatures" and "Marath's other creatures" as Open (D-7, D-8). Option 2 — keep §3's wording and add S53 as a partial answer. Option 1 preferred: the Statement is explicit and later.

3. **Two sources of the Whisp's filling, or one?** Session 01 has the Whisp fill through self-disclosure and the dissolved assessment (S17–S18, the senses); S57 has it fill with what Mara releases (the map). The S57 challenge asks whether these are one channel or two and was not answered by a Statement; Claude's reading ("two channels: *who I am* and *what has had me*") is Held-only. This blocks a single true sentence "the Whisp fills by …". *Suggested ruling:* Option 1 — two channels, both by the FIM's growing (disclosure fills the senses; Mara's release fills the map), stated as such. Option 2 — one channel: every disclosure is a piece of Mara made visible. Option 1 preferred: it keeps a Big-5 trait distinct from a Big Assumption, which ITC requires, and it matches DS-7's separate being-state dimensions. The DS-7 spec consequence (a second being-state dimension) follows the ruling.

4. **"Empties" vs "internalised" (S54) is resolved by S56–S57, but S54's Held bridge and the S54 challenge 1 wording survive in the log.** *Suggested ruling:* write the S56 form only — Mara's authority empties, the knowledge of one's limits is kept — and do not reproduce the "content goes away" reading.

5. **Where the Whisp's wisdom comes from.** S54's newborn image implies innate wisdom ("the child is born wise and unlimited" — S54 challenge 4 flagged this as a claim about human nature the growth core does not make). S67 gives a different source: the future whole self. *Suggested ruling:* the wisdom is the future self's (S67, later and explicit); the newborn image describes content and limits only (empty and unwritten, then written, then free again), not the wisdom. Say this on purpose so the bible does not inherit a claim about children being born wise.

6. **"Clone", "tool for the system to extract personality" (S2) vs "never uses its human as a tool" (S3), S68 and the VISION.** S2's own words call the Whisp "like a 'tool' for the FringeIsland system to extract the personality of its connected human" and "a clear and aware clone". The beings core already dropped both phrasings; the thinkers research repeats the extraction framing ("it is how the platform extracts the FIM's character"). *Suggested ruling:* retire "clone" and "extraction tool" as phrasings; state the mechanism as the two parallel completions (S2 Held) and the filling-by-growth (S17). The data-side truth (the profile crystallises at metamorphosis, ADR-U031) belongs to the privacy chapter, not to the Whisp's nature.

7. **The Gimbal as "device", "mobile app", "mech-lab pod" (S10, S22 Held, S26) vs the Gimbal as an equipment profile (ADR-U025).** The Whisp-and-Gimbal pairing is settled (Whisp = faculty, Gimbal = what opens the Shimmer), but the older text calls the Gimbal a device or app. *Suggested ruling:* in the beings chapter say "the Gimbal (the senses surface)" and never "the app"; ADR-U025 binds.

8. **The old research framing "the Whisp is the FIM's future self — amnesiac, but master of the craft" and the concealed cosmology (a future power harnessing consciousness; the Whisp as its unwitting instrument; embodiment as liberation; the villain as silent hero).** Session 03 adopted the future-self origin in a different form: the FIM's *own* future whole self (S67), wisdom chosen not lost (S71), the saviour human not AI (S69), mission growth not conclusion (S68). The research's "instrument of a villain" strand contradicts S3 ("never uses its human as a tool") and S69; "lost all memory" contradicts S71 ("the emptiness is chosen, not a lack"). Sources: research--growth--thinkers §The Whisp's Nature, §CONCEALED COSMOLOGY vs S67–S71. *Suggested ruling:* the bible states S67–S71 and marks the research's future-power / villain / silent-hero strand as unadopted (see C-2); "embodiment" is not adopted as a term — canon's word is "internalised" (S7).

9. **"The Whisp borrows no authority from the backstory" is Held-only.** It appears under the S69 challenge and in the Session 03 patterns, and the brief lists it among the S66–S71 material, but no Statement confirms it. *Suggested ruling:* adopt — it is the only reading under which S69 ("the saviour is your own future self") holds in the Whisp's speech and not only in the lore; PRINCIPLES-AI (no AI anchoring) points the same way. Mark it as a rule for the Whisp's voice.

10. **Mists and the future self.** Every entrant has a Whisp (S39, S48), so a Mist also has a future self reaching back; S67 challenge 3 says "consistent, but worth saying" — not said. *Suggested ruling:* adopt by consequence; the bible states it once in A10.

11. **Whether "sent back in time" survives as lore, and whether time is an axis of the cosmology.** S66 was held as a candidate; S67 reframes what was sent; S66 challenge 7 (time is new to the cosmology; whether the far side or beyond of the warm place *is* the future) is unanswered. *Suggested ruling:* the bible states only what S67–S72 lock (a future whole self reaching back; possible, not guaranteed) and marks the time axis Open (D-15). It should not say the Whisp "was sent" by anyone.

12. **The zone-fence rule "voluntariness sets the ceiling, care sets the floor" originates in Claude's S58 challenge.** Stefan's S58 ends "Or?"; the refinement was adopted only through S74 ("yes it matches" on Claude's reading, which restates it). *Suggested ruling:* treat as settled via S74 and cite S74; do not cite S58 alone for it.

13. **How firmly "a friend can stand with you against your Shadow" is locked.** S53 challenge 2 held it as a candidate; S62's Held gives it "its channel" as a consequence of the branch rule and cord-healing (S27, S36); Stefan's "lock it in" was on the form-and-force refinement. *Suggested ruling:* Option 1 — settled by consequence (the branch is the channel for seeing and helping, S36/S37, and S62 puts the Shadow under that rule). Option 2 — Open until stated. Option 1 preferred, with the wording "along a shared branch a friend can help" and nothing more specific (what "help" mechanically is stays Open, D-9).

14. **The privacy model says "Cord health and the Whisp's filling" are visible to a friend along a grown branch.** S37 and cosmology §5 give the branch-visible signal as cord *health* (colour, integrity); the Whisp's *filling* is content, and S62 extends the same rule to the Shadow (form and force, never content). *Suggested ruling:* what a friend sees along a branch is health / form and force; filling and content are never visible except by the FIM's own disclosure. Correct the privacy-model line when it is folded into the bible.

15. **S53's "maybe" — is "Shadow" also the class of Marath's creatures?** Left hedged by Stefan; S53 Held fixes "per-FIM figure only" until stated. *Suggested ruling:* rule it now: Option 1 — "Shadow" names only Mara's per-FIM form; Marath's other creatures are something else (see B-16). Option 2 — "Shadow" is the class. Option 1 preferred: it keeps the Whisp / Mara / Shadow triad clean and matches S62 (a Shadow is one FIM's inner life made visible, which a class of creatures could not be).

16. **Are Shadows NPCs? Are Marath's other creatures NPCs?** The sources are silent. A Shadow is a per-FIM manifestation of Mara, not an authored composite, so it is not an NPC under S30's definition; Marath's other creatures (S53) and the androids (S32) may be NPCs. *Suggested ruling:* state that a Shadow is a third kind of being — neither FIM nor NPC — and that Marath's other inhabitants are NPCs unless a later session says otherwise. Needs Stefan's word.

17. **S60 "not possible" needs one mechanical reading.** Claude's held reading: the beyond is unanchorable (no seed holds in Mara undiluted, S59 challenge 2), so a Whisp there hangs on the full cord with nothing to fall back to but the beginning — "not possible" = not survivable for anyone with Mara left. Not locked. *Suggested ruling:* adopt as the reading, or leave the beyond a mystery (S55 keeps it open on purpose). Either is consistent; the bible should not state a mechanism until ruled. Belongs jointly to the worlds chapter.

18. **Marath as the dark future at civilisational scale.** S66 connection (b) and S70 challenge (2) offer Marath as Mara at civilisational scale and its beyond as the collapse future; Stefan did not confirm. *Suggested ruling:* keep Open (D-16); the bible states Marath as Mara's manifestation (S51) only.

19. **The Whisp reads the FIM "in real time" and "can decline a depth the FIM asked for".** Real-time reading is S19 Held (Stefan said "cautiously pushed"); declining is S74 (adopted). How the Whisp reads the gradient (behaviour, explicit signals, biometrics) is Open (D-25). *Suggested ruling:* state the stance (the Whisp reads and nudges, and holds the fence) and leave the sensing mechanism Open.

20. **Candidate B item G ("the Whisp was designed for place 2 without knowing about place 3; the Whisp does not know it") contradicts Session 02–03.** The Whisp is exactly what walks into Marath and meets the Shadow (S61); Marath is Mara's manifestation (S51); the Whisp's wisdom is the future whole self's, which has been through it (S67). *Suggested ruling:* drop item G's claim about the Whisp (see C-4).

21. **The Whisp and the Kegan orders — S66 says the Whisp exists to move FIMs into higher orders of mind; S58 (via S74) says the platform never labels a FIM's order.** Not a contradiction, but a sentence about "higher orders" needs the framing discipline attached. *Suggested ruling:* state the purpose in the universe's own words (grow into wholeness; see one's immunities) with Kegan as substrate, and never as a claim to move or measure orders.

22. **The senses model is "canon in structure" only.** The specific Big-5-to-sense mapping, binary vs gradient coupling, and what "requiring a sense" means perceptually are all open (Session 01 threads). *Suggested ruling:* the bible states the metaphor and the motivation engine (A3) and marks the mapping Open (D-19–D-21); do not invent a mapping.

23. **The Whisp's asymmetry stated three ways.** S3 ("handicap due to initial emptiness, but super intelligent about adult development"), S39 ("empty in the sense of being 'me'"), S54 ("empty of content but full of wisdom"). *Suggested ruling:* use S54's phrasing as the canonical formulation; the other two are the same fact.

24. **Old README phrasings recorded as divergences (Session 01 §Divergences): "each FIM's personal future self"; "instrument (not mirror), fullness-as-fidelity, dual nature (encounter + companion), operational across all three worlds".** "Personal future self" is now reconciled by S67; "instrument (not mirror)" collides with the research's "growth mirror"; "fullness-as-fidelity" survives as the filling; "all three worlds" is the retired Three Worlds model. *Suggested ruling:* the bible uses none of these labels; it says what the Whisp is in Statement words.

---

### C. Candidates touching this chapter (Part 2 A/B/C and unadopted research syntheses)

1. **Candidate B item A — the Gimbal as ancient knowledge, "A(G)I as implementation": the knowledge of the fringe is old (shamanism, lucid dreaming, OBE, sleep-paralysis folklore); what was missing was implementation; AI closed the gap; the Gimbal is a culmination, not a discovery.** Against Session 03: it is about the Gimbal (instrument), not the Whisp, so it does not by itself put AI in the saviour role (S69) — but it hands AI the decisive move in the universe's origin story, while S69 keeps "the tool that could run away with the future is used by humans to hear their own wiser voice" and S76 makes the myth discovered, not told. It also carries a research-programme / past-present-future lineage (Candidate B F–G; thinkers research §three-time-period) whose "future power" slot is now filled differently by S67. *Suggested ruling:* keep open, as worlds/equipment mythology, not a beings fact; if adopted, the framing note ("AI finished something ancient; the universe stays experiential, not technological, at its root") must be read with S69 so that the Whisp's origin (the FIM's own future self) is never attributed to the AI. Session 03 lists this reconciliation for the rulings list.

2. **Thinkers research — "The Whisp is the FIM's future self, amnesiac but master of the craft" + the concealed cosmology (future power, the Whisp as its unwitting instrument, embodiment as liberation, the villain as silent hero, the single-consciousness loop).** Partly converges with S67 (future self) and S71 (wisdom, not content), but "lost all memory" ≠ "chosen emptiness", and the villain-instrument strand contradicts S3, S68, S69. The "single-consciousness loop" (FIM, Whisp and the power as one being at three distances) is the nearest thing to S67 and is explicitly unlocked. *Suggested ruling:* drop the villain / instrument / silent-hero strand; note "future self" and the loop as lineage that S67 made canon in its own form; do not adopt "embodiment" (canon: "internalised", S7).

3. **Candidate B item E — the three altered states: lucid dreaming lands in place 2, out-of-body in place 3, sleep paralysis is being caught in the Shimmer; the sleep-paralysis "presence" maps onto the membrane, not onto a place.** Touches the beings chapter twice: (a) S47 already made the sleep-paralysis menace the Shadow, and S53 made the Shadow Mara's form met *in Marath* — so item E's "presence caught in the Shimmer" and "Shadow as the Shimmer's own character" sit differently from S53; (b) OBE "you are through but exposed, observing from outside yourself" describes the FIM travelling, whereas S61 has the Whisp travel while the FIM stays body-side. Session 03 open threads list both. *Suggested ruling:* keep open for the worlds chapter; for the beings chapter, S53 and S61 bind: the Shadow is met in Marath through the Whisp.

4. **Candidate B item G — the ball-cord-Whisp system was designed for place 2, blind to place 3; the Whisp's modelling posture may be wrong or naive in place 3; "the Whisp does not know it".** Contradicts S51, S56, S61, S67 (see B-20). *Suggested ruling:* drop the Whisp claim; whatever survives of item G is worlds mythology.

5. **Candidate B item B/D — the ball as key, the ball maturing "as the Whisp develops".** Adjacent only: D ties the ball's capability to the Whisp's filling and the cord dial (S40). S37 forbids brightness-as-fullness; nothing in Sessions 02–03 touches it. *Suggested ruling:* keep open for the worlds chapter; the beings chapter says nothing about the ball's maturity.

6. **Candidate C — naming the warm world from the Whisp's voice.** Adopted only in its method (S65) and its bar as far as Stefan stated it: a positive, growth-oriented, tough-love voice (S63); "warmth first, challenge second" and "wisdom-that-encourages, not bliss" are Claude's cautions (S63 challenge 4, S65) that the journey proceeded on. The many characterisations of the Whisp in the tables (good friend who admonishes, warden, counsel, the thought that flies out and returns, fearlessness, the true voice against the deceiving one) are sounding-board fits, not properties of the Whisp. The eighth-pass reading of place 2's rest as "availability, not occupancy" and S78's butterfly image touch the Whisp's stance on rest but are not locked. Note also the open question whether the brief describes the Whisp's voice or the felt safety of the village (fourth pass). *Suggested ruling:* adopt nothing about the Whisp from Candidate C beyond S63's own words; keep the naming journey open (worlds chapter).

7. **Thinkers research — Boundary principle "philosophical companion and growth mirror, not a therapeutic tool"; in clinical distress the Whisp is honest about its limits and points toward human support.** The "not a therapy service" half is constitutional (VISION §Ecosystem boundaries); the "growth mirror" label and the clinical-distress behaviour are not in any Statement. *Suggested ruling:* adopt the boundary (already constitutional); mark the distress behaviour as an open rule for the Whisp's specification (D-27); do not adopt "growth mirror" as a name.

8. **Thinkers research — "Tough love: the Whisp holds the call in silence" (refusing to rescue; the call toward Live/Grow/Matter never goes silent and the patience never runs out).** Tough love itself is S3 (a Statement, earlier than the research). The silence/refusing-to-rescue formulation is not in a Statement; S74's holding-environment reading (confirmation and contradiction) is the adopted theory of tough love. *Suggested ruling:* not adopted; keep as research lineage under S3 and S74.

9. **Thinkers research — "The Three-Level Shadow — a cosmological hypothesis" (individual Shadow = the FIM's Unintegrated Self projected; pair Shadow; collective Shadow; the lost travellers' material in the collective level).** Not adopted by any Statement. S53 gives the Shadow a different substrate (Mara, the negative inner voice; S56: the immune system in the ITC sense), one per FIM, met by branch-mates (S62). The pair and community levels have no Statement; S66 connection (b) offered a civilisational scale, unconfirmed. The research's own terminology rule (never "shadow" for Jung; write "the Unintegrated Self") is research-level, not canon. *Suggested ruling:* drop the Jungian origin as the Shadow's mechanism; keep pair/community Shadows Open (D-13) if Stefan wants them at all; the substrate of record is Mara / ITC.

10. **Thinkers research — "The Whisp models what the FIM is growing toward" (a modeling relationship, not instruction; cord length as readiness to receive modelling) and the Behavioral Specification list.** The modelling-relationship idea is echoed in S47/S48 Held ("the modeling-companion", "the modeling relationship that defines the universe") and Candidate B D cites S40 for cord length as readiness — so it is Held vocabulary, not a Statement. The behavioural list (holds "Maybe", reflects without naming, refuses premature closure, etc.) is not adopted. *Suggested ruling:* keep as the Whisp-specification research base; the bible can say the Whisp embodies rather than teaches only if Stefan rules it.

11. **Thinkers research — "Mirror questions" (Who am I not? …) as a map of the Unintegrated Self.** Not adopted. *Suggested ruling:* keep open as Whisp-dialogue craft; ties to nothing settled.

12. **Kegan research §4.2–4.3 — ITC as a core platform mechanic (the four-column map as a journey threshold beat) and the Whisp's respawn as an immunity encounter.** Adopted in substance by S56–S57 (the Whisp holds the map) and S74 (the learning zone = the test run in Marath with respawn); not adopted as the specific journey design or the respawn-time diagnostic message. §4.1's "the Whisp's own growth trajectory mirrors Order 3 → 5" and §4.6 are not adopted and sit badly with "never labels order". *Suggested ruling:* cite S56/S57/S74 as the adoption; leave the journey-beat design to the story chapter; drop the Whisp-mirrors-orders idea.

13. **Kegan research §4.4 — the holding environment (confirmation, contradiction, continuity) as the Whisp's theory of tough love; "the Whisp remains through failures, the holding environment that never abandons".** Adopted through S74 support (b). *Suggested ruling:* settled as substrate, cite S74.

14. **S78 — the butterfly rests in the rain (rest is legitimate; rest protects what flies; the return is built in).** Offered as an image; Claude's candidate rules (rest is the Whisp's when chosen while seeing and ending in flight, Mara's when avoidance never ends; the FIM decides the storm has passed and the Whisp asks "is it still raining, or is it Mara?"; rest carries no cost in the world) are not locked. *Suggested ruling:* keep open (D-28); the bible may carry the image only if Stefan rules it.

Merged with D-12.
