### E. Glossary terms for this chapter

- **the Whisp** — each person's own inner dialogue oriented toward growth and compassion; the person's own future, whole self reaching back; also the avatar through which the FIM walks the Fringe on the cord. Empty of content, full of wisdom. [S1, S4, S22, S54, S67]
- **Mara** — the negative inner voice, obeyed unknowingly; the person's immune system in the Immunity-to-Change sense; full of content at the start, its authority empties as the Whisp fills. Manifested as Marath. [S51, S54, S56]
- **the Shadow** — what Mara looks like when met in Marath; one per FIM; grows with depth into Marath; visible to others only along a shared branch, as form and force, never content. [S53, S59, S62]
- **Mist** — the anonymous entrant: a translucent, drifting becoming-figure in the hyaline state, with its own Whisp and unpaid-out cord; near-side only; ephemeral and unlinkable. [S47, S48; ADR-U031]
- **hyaline state** — the translucent, unanchored, not-yet-committed condition a Mist is in (the Mist is the figure; the hyaline state the condition). [S47; ADR-U031]
- **accretion** — a Mist filling in as it answers the founding questions: eyes, mouth and features, definition, condensation; tracks progress, never trait. [S47; ADR-U031]
- **FIM** — FringeIsland Member: the base identity, a person with a Whisp, with their equal place in the Tree. "Member" is the platform synonym only. [roles core L0]
- **transcendence / metamorphosis** — one event, two names (platform / lore): the Mist condensing into the anchored glowing ball when all founding questions are answered AND consent is given; the first paying-out of the cord. [S47, S48; ADR-U031]
- **the delivery** — metamorphosis seen from the Whisp's side: the Whisp born into separateness across a kept cord (parturition, not genesis). [S48]
- **the cord** — the FIM–Whisp lifeline, FIM at the origin, Whisp at the far end; kept, not severed; its length is the FIM's dial of voluntariness and developmental intimacy; carries salience (maturity) and health (colour, integrity). [S27, S37, S40, S48]
- **the Void** — the axis of separation: wherever the Whisp is once it leaves the FIM, along the whole cord. [S27; cosmology §5]
- **the senses** — the five Big-5-mapped metaphorical perception channels that open as the FIM discloses to the Whisp; the world requires all five for full experience. [S18]
- **the map** — what hinders the FIM (their competing commitments and Big Assumptions); held by the Whisp, filled as Mara releases it. [S56, S57]
- **the transfer** — the Whisp's filling and Mara's emptying as one process seen from two ends. [S57]
- **the zones** — comfort → fear → learning → growth, with panic as the fence; the felt experience of the transfer. [S73, S74]
- **the panic fence** — the boundary the Whisp never opens, even on request: voluntariness sets the ceiling, care sets the floor. [S19, S74]
- **tough love** — the Whisp's stance: always wants its human well, never at the cost of avoiding what holds them back; disagrees with compassion. [S3]
- **internalised** — the endgame: the Whisp (and Mara) integral to the human, no longer separate, FringeIsland no longer needed; the FIM is whole. [S6, S7, S54]
- **the backdrop** — the discovered, never-told myth: the Whisp as the FIM's own future whole self reaching back; the future at stake; possible, not guaranteed. [S66–S72, S76]
- **the conviction** — the founders' public why in the MANIFESTO preamble; names no myth. [S76, S77]
- **NPC** — a world-inhabitant composed of up to three authorial layers (body — Creator; culture — Anthropologist; character — Teller), added in proportion to the depth its role demands; no Whisp. [S30]
- **Jake-and-Avatar structure** — the FIM safe body-side, the Whisp the projection that can be harmed and respawns; two views of one being. [S22]
- **meta-safety** — real felt stakes inside a frame that guarantees the human cannot be broken; harm reaches the Whisp, never the FIM. [S19, S22, S27]
- **DS-7 Intelligence / DS-1 World Model** — the two services that own the Whisp's being-face and world-presence face respectively; the AI is the medium through which the future self speaks. [ADR-U029, S69]

#### Retired or superseded names

- **AI Mentor / Mentor** (for the AI companion) → **the Whisp**. [CQ-009 note; beings core]
- **Shadow** (as the anonymous entrant, S16/S39/S45/S46) → **Mist**; "Shadow" reassigned to Mara's form in Marath. [S47, S53; ADR-U031]
- **Visitor** (platform term) → **Mist**. [roles core; ADR-U004 → U027 → U031]
- **Guest** (in-session suggestion that a Shadow could visit the village as a guest) → wrong; the village is FIM-only. [S45]
- **"each FIM's personal future self"** (old README) → reconciled as **the FIM's own future, whole self reaching back**. [S67]
- **"clone" / "tool for the system to extract personality"** (S2 wording) → the two parallel completions; filled by the human growing. [S2 Held, S17; B-6]
- **"the Whisp is the FringeIsland mobile app's faculty" / the Gimbal as a device or app** (S10, S22, S26) → the Gimbal is the **senses-surface equipment profile**. [ADR-U025]
- **"affordance"** → **equipment**. [ADR-U025]
- **"amnesiac future self", "embodiment", "the future power", "the silent hero", "the Unintegrated Self", "growth mirror", "instrument (not mirror)"** (research and old README vocabulary) → not canon; the Whisp's origin is S67, its endgame "internalised" (S7), its counterpart Mara (S51).
- **Three Worlds / Safe Harbour / The Other Side; "the Beyond" for the far side** → Ordinary World → the Shimmer → two places, each with near side / far side / beyond. [cosmology core §What this supersedes; S52, S55]
- **Makers / Weavers / Skalds** → retired; the Dreamineer specialisations are Creator, Anthropologist, Teller, Wayfinder. [beings core; roles core]
- **"Agents, not scenery"** (S15 flat reading of NPCs) → layered, depth-on-demand composites; some NPCs are scenery-with-belonging by design. [S30]


# Chapter map — growth

Chapter scope: the three questions × the Three Perspectives and the 9-cell matrix; Live / Grow / Matter and the framing discipline; the engagement spectrum and the member archetypes; the growth gradient (zones), the panic fence, voluntariness/care; ITC scaling and the Mara-to-Whisp transfer as growth; graduation; meta-safety and respawn as what makes the test safe; the privacy model; the research anchors.

Sources read: canon--growth--how-growth-works.md, canon--growth--privacy-model.md, canon--cosmology--worlds-topology.md (§5, §7, §8, §10, §11), canon--narrative--how-story-works.md (Respawn), canon--beings--whisp-and-npcs.md, VISION.md, MANIFESTO.md, ADR-U031, discovery Part 1–3 (S1–S9, S12, S17–S22, S26–S28, S37–S40, S43, S46–S48, S49–S65, S66–S78, the 2026-09-17 tags on Session 01 patterns and open threads), questions--ecosystem-open-questions.md, record--universe-to-spec-manifestation.md, research--growth--kegan-immunity-to-change.md (§1.3, §4, §5), research--growth--thinkers-and-models-behind-the-whisp.md (mission, design/boundary principles, syntheses).

Citation forms: [S<n>] a discovery statement; [growth core §<heading>] canon--growth--how-growth-works.md; [privacy model] canon--growth--privacy-model.md; [cosmology §n]; [narrative Respawn]; [beings core]; [VISION …]; [MANIFESTO …]; [ADR-U031]; [kegan §n] and [thinkers …] the two research reports (never canon; cited only where a statement adopted them).

---

### A. Settled — what the bible can state as true

#### A1. The purpose: self-awareness, then graduation

- FringeIsland is an ecosystem built around three questions: Who am I? What do I want? How do I get there? [VISION Purpose; S6]
- The platform does not answer the questions; it holds them — space, structure and companionship — so each member finds their own answers. [VISION Purpose; VISION principle 1, 5]
- What the universe is for is that FIMs become more self-aware — internal self-awareness (who they are inside) and external self-awareness (how they appear and operate in the world) — about who they are, what they want and how they get there, so that they move forward in life deliberately. [S6]
- Over time the experience internalises: the member carries their own wiser inner voice into ordinary life and no longer needs the platform. That is success. FringeIsland is built to graduate, not retain. [S6, S7; VISION Purpose, principle 7; beings core "Internalised by design"]
- At the end the Whisp is an integral part of the human, without the need for FringeIsland. [S7]
- Wholeness is two-sided: the FIM is "whole" when the Whisp has filled and Mara has emptied — both voices internalised. [S54, S57]
- The arc is a return: the newborn is unwritten and free; experience writes limits into the person (Mara fills); development cracks those limits (Mara dissolves); the adult becomes free again as the child was — now knowingly. [S54]
- The Whisp is the FIM's own future, whole self reaching back; it brings wisdom (how to see, how to meet what has you), never content (who you became, what you chose); that future is possible, not guaranteed — the FIM closes the loop by growing. [S67, S71]
- The Whisp's mission is the growth itself, never a conclusion; "choosing well" means choosing while seeing one's immunities, not choosing what the Whisp would. [S68, S72]
- Growth is delight, not deficiency: FringeIsland never runs on "you are not good enough". This inverts the mainstream self-improvement premise and is a guarded core value. [S28; VISION principle 6; growth core §Beneath the questions]
- Growth means becoming more truly yourself — clearer on who you are, what you want, where you're going — not faster, louder or more impressive. [MANIFESTO "Personal growth over performance"]
- FringeIsland is not a course platform with certificates, a social network optimised for engagement, a therapy service or clinical tool, or a gamified productivity app. [VISION Ecosystem boundaries]
- Personal development happens through lived experience, not instruction: entertainment is the surface, development the consequence; developmental themes stay invisible in the foundation layer. [VISION What this is, principle 2; growth core §Research foundation ("never surfaced explicitly, always present structurally")]
- Experiential knowledge is not reducible to information: being told is not the same as living it — which is why the assessments dissolve into dialogue, why the world is felt through senses, and why the backdrop survives being spoiled. [S18; S75 point 5; S76 (locked); Session 01 open-threads "Lineage breadcrumb" (principle kept, citation parked)]

#### A2. The three questions × the Three Perspectives; the 9-cell matrix

- Each question exists across three perspectives — Individual (1), Relationship (1+1), Collective (1+Community) — three simultaneous angles on the same human, not three separate domains. [S9; VISION Structural concepts "Three Perspectives"; growth core §The red thread]
- Three questions × three perspectives give the 9-cell matrix, a structural description of the developmental space FringeIsland holds — not a curriculum. Individual pieces of content may touch one cell, several, or all nine. [growth core §The 9-Cell Matrix, §How this connects]
- Who am I? — Individual: values, strengths, blind spots, edges (reflection, assessment, journaling); Relational: how I show up with another, what patterns I carry (pair work, feedback, encounter); Communal: what role I play and what I contribute in the groups I belong to (group dynamics, belonging, contribution). "Not tests that categorise you, but tools that illuminate you." [growth core §Who am I?]
- What do I want? — Individual: what matters to me, what I move toward (purpose, values, life direction); Relational: what connections I want to build (relational intention, attachment, intimacy, trust); Communal: what world I want to help create (collective vision, social purpose, civic imagination). The difference between what you have been told to want and what actually calls you. [growth core §What do I want?]
- How do I get there? — Individual: the practices and disciplines that carry me (habits, skills, discipline); Relational: how I learn to be present with another (listening, conflict, vulnerability); Communal: how I contribute to something larger (leadership, service, stewardship, collective action). [growth core §How do I get there?]
- The matrix makes the space visible so that journey designers cover all perspectives, the Whisp orients to the perspective the FIM is currently engaging, and seasons and episodes can move FIMs across perspectives over time. [growth core §How this connects]
- The individual perspective can be worked alone; the relational and communal perspectives structurally require genuine human connection — other people, not NPC interactions, not AI-generated activity. A member who never engages with others can still grow, but the full developmental arc opens only through connection. [growth core §The developmental honesty; VISION principle 3 "Developmental interdependence"]
- The same three perspectives run through respawn (alone, with a travelling partner, with a group) [S19; narrative Respawn], through cooperative cord-healing (relationships and community keep your Whisp alive when you are stretched thin) [S27, S36, S37; cosmology §7], through the Tree (a 1+1 branch is a single bough; a 1+community bond is a braid where several balls' branches gather) [S38], and through the dark branch used for growth "as individual + relationship + society" [S73].
- Matter recurs in the same shape: mattering to oneself / to others / to a higher cause is the Three Perspectives again. [S28]
- The three questions are the red thread through everything — every journey, episode, group, conversation; engaged lightly or deeply, quickly or slowly, always there. [growth core §The red thread]

#### A3. Beneath the questions: Live / Grow / Matter

- Beneath the questions lie the drives — why a human bothers to ask at all: all humans want to live, to grow, and to matter. Offered as a design axiom (no hard evidence claimed), supported by convergent evidence, the three-ness chosen not derived. [S28; VISION Purpose]
- Live — present-moment engagement; enjoying the joyride, not postponing happiness to a far future; a parallel to mindfulness. [S28]
- Grow — curiosity and the urge to learn and enhance, explicitly not from deficiency; deliberate choices of action; planned happenstance (ready to jump on the train when opportunity comes). The healthiest Grow is responsiveness, not tightness: awake enough to catch the train, loose enough to enjoy the platform while waiting. [S28]
- Matter — two poles, bridged by influence. Internal pole: "I feel I matter", anchored in what I think, feel and do, within my control — the seat of freedom and security. External pole: mattering to others and to something bigger — the seat of contribution and connection, pursued through what one can offer and influence, outcomes held lightly. The internal anchor makes you free; the external reaching makes you not alone. Collapsing Matter onto the internal pole alone would be a cruel empty promise to the genuinely isolated. [S28]
- Live and Grow are a corrective pair: Live without Grow is indulgence; Grow without Live is joyless striving. [S28]
- The three dance in balance, never in sequence: none is ever complete, so there are no rungs to climb; each feeds the other two. The Whisp does not march a FIM up through them; it senses which drive is currently starved and leans the journey toward restoring balance. [S28 (resolution 2026-05-25); growth core §Beneath the questions; VISION Purpose]
- The triads nest: Live/Grow/Matter (why) under Who/What/How (the questions) under 1 / 1+1 / 1+community (the perspectives). [S28; growth core]
- The architecture already holds both poles of Matter: internal — meta-safety, the FIM who cannot be broken; external — the 1+1 / 1+community layer, FIMs healing each other's cords, Dreamineers contributing to the world. [S28, S27, S13]

#### A4. The framing discipline: informed by, never equal to

- Live/Grow/Matter is the plain-language, human-facing aspiration, owned by FringeIsland; the science beneath — SDT, PERMA+, the Big Five, Immunity to Change, the flourishing dimensions — is the substrate: borrowed, cited, never claimed as FringeIsland's own. [S28; growth core §Framing discipline]
- The one rule: always say Live/Grow/Matter is informed by / resonant with / draws on the science — never equals or maps onto it. Claim kinship, never equivalence. Convergence-without-collapse is the evidence the three words point at something real. [S28]
- The same discipline governs the zones and Immunity to Change: the universe speaks in its own language (Mara, the Whisp, the zones); ITC is the validated substrate underneath. [S56 framing note; S73 challenge 2 and S74 framing note (adopted)]
- The platform never labels a FIM's developmental order; a FIM's Marath may deepen and change character over years without the universe ever saying "Order 3" or "moving to 4". [S58 refinement, cited as the rule by CQ-018 rec. 2]
- The plain-language foundation is durable: the scientific substrate can be refined or swapped as the science evolves without shaking the foundation; the drives remain accountable to the science (defensible in SDT/PERMA+ terms) but are not derived as its sum. [S28]
- The Whisp is a philosophical companion and growth mirror, not a therapeutic tool: it does not diagnose, correct or treat; in genuine clinical distress it is honest about its limits and points to human support. [VISION Ecosystem boundaries ("not a therapy service or clinical tool"); thinkers "Boundary Principle" — research, consistent with the constitutional boundary]

#### A5. How growth is registered: the Whisp fills by the human growing

- The Whisp fills by the human growing: growth is the mechanism; the Whisp's filling is the registry of it. Increased awareness is one part of growth. [S17; beings core]
- Two parallel completions: as the Whisp fills with more of the human, the human becomes more self-aware — one process seen from two angles. [S2]
- Validated instruments (Big 5 et al.) are never delivered as questionnaires; they dissolve into the Whisp's curious dialogue, distributed over time — the structure preserved, the form transformed. The FIM is not completing a profile; they are bringing their Whisp to life. [S17; beings core]
- The Whisp's quality as navigator and mediator of the world is anchored to how much the FIM has disclosed to it; the universe's responsiveness is earned by the FIM's willingness to be seen — by themselves. The five Big 5 traits map metaphorically to the five senses; the world needs all five for full experience, so the motivation to keep disclosing is intrinsic and sensory, never coerced. [S17, S18; beings core "Assessment dissolved into dialogue; the senses"]
- Multi-instrument architecture: Big 5 is the on-ramp, not the destination; other validated frameworks contribute over time. The more relevant instruments a FIM discloses across, the more custom-made their journeys become — a signature journey rather than a charter trip. [S19]
- A second channel of filling, locked in Session 02: the Whisp fills with what Mara releases — the Whisp's filling and Mara's emptying are one process seen from two ends. The Whisp is the holder of the FIM's map. [S57 ("I mean it!"), S54]

#### A6. Mara, the transfer, and Immunity to Change as growth

- The Whisp is the growth inner voice; Mara is the negative inner voice that many people listen to without knowing it. Mara does not need to be discovered to be obeyed — it needs to be discovered to be seen. [S51]
- The Whisp is at the start empty of content but full of wisdom and fills with content; Mara is full of content from the start and empties as the Whisp grows; finally Mara is internalised and the FIM is whole. [S54]
- Internalising Mara is the Immunity-to-Change move: learn and internalise what hinders you before you can change; become aware, turn it into a positive step forward, step through it — and by this the FIM empties Mara's authority to rule them. What empties is Mara's authority; what is kept is the knowledge of one's limits (Subject becomes Object). [S56]
- Marath is a manifestation of Mara; the Shadow is what Mara looks like when met in Marath — one per FIM. Healing a wound in the warm world by acting on its cause in Marath is confronting one's own negative self-talk at its source. [S51, S53; S32]
- The confrontation with Mara is always mediated through the Whisp: the FIM never faces Mara directly; the wiser voice is what walks into Marath and meets the Shadow — "that is probably the point". [S61]
- ITC scales — from the smallest change up to the magnitude of a move between orders — if the FIM so wishes and allows it. The FIM dials the depth of the invitation, never the outcome. [S58, S58 refinement (adopted via S74)]
- The Shadow's size grows with depth into Marath — how far past the Shimmer the FIM's Whisp has travelled; Marath's beyond is Mara at full strength. Its danger is relative to the FIM: unapproachable while Mara is full, approachable as it empties; it may be the last thing met before graduation. [S59, S60]
- The beyond is optional. The road there runs through every immunity the FIM carries; most FIMs will not want to go, for many it will not be possible, and nothing requires it. Graduation is not gated on the beyond; a FIM can be whole without ever having gone there. FringeIsland has no end to reach and no final boss. [S60]
- One mechanism at three scales: Mara as one person's negative voice, as the immune system that keeps a person the same, and (in the backdrop) as a collective immunity to change; personal growth and the world's future are the same mechanism at different sizes. [S66 and Session 03 patterns — the civilisational scale is backdrop, never told (S76)]
- Rest is legitimate and part of the breath-rhythm: out, back, out again; the shelter is always available (the ball one step away) and the FIM is not meant to stay in it. [S78 image; cosmology §10; S33] — see B12 on S78's status.

#### A7. The growth gradient — the zones, the fence, the dial

- Growth happens by the FIM being cautiously pushed out of the comfort zone toward growth, without getting into panic — "cautiously" is structural; the Whisp reads the FIM in real time and nudges. [S19; narrative Respawn ("the Whisp pushes cautiously toward the growth zone, never the panic zone")]
- The zones are five: comfort → fear → learning → growth, with panic past the fence. [S73, S74 (locked; refines the three-step gradient of cosmology §10 and the narrative core)]
- The zones are the felt experience of the Mara-to-Whisp transfer and map onto the four stages of Immunity to Change: comfort = unconsciously immune (Mara unseen, obeyed); fear = consciously immune (Mara seen and loudest, met as the Shadow through the Whisp); learning = consciously released (safe, modest tests run in Marath under meta-safety and respawn); growth = unconsciously released (Mara's authority emptied into the Whisp); panic = the test too big to be safe, which ITC warns snaps the immunity back harder. [S74]
- The panic fence protects the growth itself, not only the FIM: an overwhelming test confirms the Big Assumption and strengthens the immunity. The Whisp holds the fence even when a FIM asks for more depth. [S74 "Why panic matters mechanically"]
- Voluntariness sets the ceiling, care sets the floor: the FIM's wish and self-permission gate how deep the invitation goes; the never-panic-zone rule binds from the other side. [S58 refinement, adopted in S74]
- The fear zone is where Mara speaks loudest — excuses, "what will others think", the reasons not to go on — met through the Whisp; it is not a feeling the world is designed to cause, and the story never uses it to scare the FIM. [S73 ch.1 and S74 ch.1 (framing adopted with S74); S70]
- The gradient is spatial, and the distance that matters is Void distance — how far the Whisp has ventured out on the cord — not how far the body walks. "Far from home" means far out on the lifeline. Venture out for challenge, return home for safety: the developmental breath-rhythm as geography. [S26, S27; cosmology §10]
- The FIM holds the cord: the FIM decides how long it may be — pay out to venture, reel in to retreat. Risk is depth times protection (cord length × seeding); the peril is opt-in; you are never sent further than you allow. Cord length is also developmental intimacy: short = held close, long = trusted into distance. [S40, S48; cosmology §5]
- Anchoring is a discipline that encodes a growth lesson: consolidate before advancing; venturing far without anchoring is panic-zone overshoot with a concrete consequence (fall back to the last anchor, or start over). [S27; cosmology §8, §10]
- You do not enter the panic zone before you have a home to return to: the Mist's lock on the far side is intrinsic (no ball, no anchor-root), not a fence. [S39, S45, S48; cosmology §11]
- The dark branch of the backdrop is the stage and the stake of the storytelling; it never drives an individual FIM's pace or depth — no countdown, no guilt, no hurry. The FIM uses the world for growth at their own pace, with the Whisp's tough love, across the three perspectives and the zones. [S70, S73]
- Kegan's holding environment — confirmation, contradiction, continuity — is the theory beneath the Whisp's tough love: too little contradiction keeps the FIM in comfort, too little confirmation tips them into panic. Informed by, not equal to. [S74 support (b), adopted; kegan §1.3, §4.4]

#### A8. Meta-safety and respawn — what makes the test safe

- FringeIsland is safe: the world contains threat; the experience of being in the world does not. Real felt stakes inside a frame that guarantees reversibility. [S19; narrative Respawn]
- If the Whisp and its human "get into trouble and die", it is simple to rewind and redo — respawn, to try again until the skill is mastered or the story's task accomplished, alone, with a travelling partner, or with a group. Productive failure as a structural affordance. [S19; narrative Respawn]
- The FIM and Whisp are Jake and his Avatar: the human is never the thing at risk; the Whisp is what walks the world, can be harmed, dies in a story and respawns; the FIM is the constant point of consciousness. Felt stakes do not require existential risk — Jake still does not want his Avatar hurt. [S22; beings core "The avatar"]
- Harm travels the bond toward the delivered one (the Whisp), never back to the source (the FIM). [S48]
- Cutting the cord endangers the Whisp, never the FIM; severance recovery is two-tier (last anchor / start over) and recovery is reunion. [S27; cosmology §5, §8]
- The loop is the medium, not a safety net: what persists across a loop — tactical knowledge, relational insight, emotional clarity — is part of what the story is about; respawn is plural (event-local, round-bounded, day-bounded, episode-bounded), stays inside the story, and is nested and scaled (small failures → home base; large → episode/season repeat). [S20, S21; narrative Respawn]
- Failure is one of growth's most reliable teachers: nothing is permanent, everything is tryable, every wrong turn becomes wisdom carried forward. [MANIFESTO "Safe experimentation over fear of failure"]
- The world is gardened, not guarded: nothing is permanently destroyed; the worst case is "not yet grown" or "receded", recoverable the moment care resumes — this keeps faith with growth as delight. [S34, S35; cosmology §9]
- Meta-safety and respawn are exactly what make Marath usable as the testing ground for one's Big Assumptions: ITC's tests must be safe, modest and actionable, and nothing there can break the FIM. [S56 ch.1 (adopted through S74's learning-zone row)]

#### A9. The engagement spectrum

- There are two different ways of engaging in FringeIsland as a FIM: cultivate your FIM home, or go on expeditions and explore near or far from it. This is about how a FIM engages, not about the community that creates the experience (roles). [growth core header note (Stefan, 2026-09-16)]
- Members engage along a natural spectrum; no position is more valid than another; every path leads to growth; there is no progression requirement — a Homebody is not "behind" an Explorer; members move freely. [growth core §The engagement spectrum]
- Homebody — tends the inner garden, builds the digital home, reflects quietly, never ventures far into the wider narrative; can do deep individual work (the 1 perspective) through reflection, journaling, solo journeys. A genuinely valuable, complete mode of engagement — not lesser or preliminary. [growth core §The Spectrum, §Implications]
- Explorer — deep in narrative arcs, seasonal episodes, quests, group dynamics, community contribution, world-building; living in FringeIsland as a genuine second world; naturally engages 1+1 and 1+community. [growth core §The Spectrum]
- The far pole — those who press further still, far out on the cord into the deep reaches: the growth-bearing Void distance. [growth core §The Spectrum "Beyond"] — name collision, see B5.
- Solo tools are as important as social features; community and group features are not add-ons — they are the only path to the relational and communal perspectives. [growth core §Implications]
- No rankings, no counts, no comparative progress anywhere in the universe: all balls glow equal; the crown is ambient; branch and cord signals are legible only to their endpoints. [S37, S38; cosmology §6–7]

#### A10. The privacy model

- What happens on FringeIsland is yours: your inner journey, your discoveries, the person you are becoming — you decide what stays private and what you share, with whom and when. A founding principle, not a policy. Member data serves the member, not the platform; the platform never profits from what you entrust to it. [privacy model §Principle; MANIFESTO "Member privacy over commercial opportunity"; VISION principle 4]
- Three tiers of visibility: private by default; selectively shared; transparently shared. [privacy model §Three Tiers]
- Private by default, no opt-in required: the inner journey and self-discovery progress; home and garden (the inviolable ball, the FIM holds the only key); journal entries; assessment results and developmental data; cord health and the Whisp's filling. [privacy model; S34, S43; cosmology §6]
- Selectively shared: the FIM chooses what, to whom, when — specific individuals (1+1) or groups (1+community); per aspect or per region of the home (share the garden without the journal; open one room to a friend and keep the rest locked); always voluntary; revocable. [privacy model; S43, S44]
- Transparently shared: special opt-in groups (accountability circles, growth cohorts, mastermind groups, facilitated group journeys) where members agree to a norm of openness — explicit informed consent from every participant, never the default; even there the FIM retains control over what they contribute. [privacy model]
- Structural implications: the platform never surfaces private data without explicit FIM action; sharing controls are granular (per aspect, per audience, per timeframe); Group Stewards cannot see members' private developmental data; no aggregate derived from private journeys is visible to other FIMs; anonymised aggregates only with explicit informed consent, responsible handling, clear member benefit, under enterprise stewardship (Universeers, Council, Foundation). [privacy model §Structural implications]
- What is intimate is seen only along the bond that can help it: cord health is visible to a friend only along a grown branch — glanceable not diagnostic, invited not imposed, self first (the FIM's own Whisp is the first responder). [S37; cosmology §5, §7]
- Another FIM's Shadow manifests to you only along a shared branch (who sees), and what a friend sees is its form and force — that you are struggling and how heavily — never its content (what your Mara says), which only the FIM can disclose. Strangers see no Shadow. [S62 (locked with refinement)]
- A Mist's own data is ephemeral: anonymous auth, no PII, erased soon after inactivity or explicit close; presence is session-ephemeral and unlinkable; no trait-profile is computed before consent; every anonymous exchange is live and unrecorded; transcendence (metamorphosis) is the persistence-and-consent threshold and the migration is atomic. Accretion tracks progress, never trait — stage is legible, content is not. [S46, S47; ADR-U031; privacy model]
- Becoming a FIM does not unlock the ability to interact; it unlocks the ability for interaction to be remembered. [S47; ADR-U031]

#### A11. Research anchors (names only, as substrate)

- The Whisp draws on the project's flourishing research: the dual-continuum finding (mental health and illness as separate dimensions — flourishing must be actively built), the multi-system requirement (no single axis), the Three Perspectives meta-lens, and Immunity to Change; the two anchors Stefan lifted forward are the Three Perspectives and Immunity to Change. [S8, S9; beings core "Empty at first"]
- The Whisp is fluent in both: the Three Perspectives as the lens across all of life; ITC as why genuine growth is hard and what dialogue can unlock — change is a developmental problem, not a willpower problem; the old behaviour does real protective work. [S9]
- The named research base woven into the experience layer: Big 5/OCEAN, VIA, Erin Meyer's Culture Map; Frankl, Steger, ikigai; SDT (Deci & Ryan), Ryff, Keyes; Kegan; Baumeister & Leary, attachment theory, the Harvard Study; Bandura. [growth core §Research foundation]
- Kinship examples (illustrative, not 1:1): Live ~ PERMA Positive emotion and Engagement; Grow ~ SDT Competence and Autonomy, PERMA Accomplishment; Matter ~ SDT Relatedness, PERMA Relationships and Meaning. [S28]

---

### B. Rulings needed

1. **Three zones vs five.** Cosmology §10, the narrative core and the graduation tracker row all state comfort → growth → panic; S73–S74 lock comfort → fear → learning → growth with panic as the fence. Sources: cosmology §10, narrative Respawn, tracker row 1 | S73, S74. Suggested ruling: adopt S74 (five zones, panic the fence) everywhere; retire the three-step phrase. Why: S74 is the latest statement, explicitly "refines the three-step gradient … at ratification".

2. **Is the five-zone gradient one spatial band per FIM, or a zone per Big Assumption?** S74's framing note (adopted) puts the zone bands on the Void-distance gradient beside the Shadow's growth with depth (S59); S74 challenge 2 (unanswered) says a FIM is in a zone per assumption, not as a whole, which argues against any single zone meter. Sources: S26, S27, S59, S74 note | S74 ch.2. Suggested ruling: Option 1 — depth on the cord is the FIM's *exposure* (how much of Mara they are letting be met), while the zone is felt per assumption; the bible states both without a zone meter. Option 2 — one gradient per FIM, simpler, contradicts ITC. Why: Option 1 keeps S58 (ITC scales from one assumption) and S74 coherent and forbids a visible "zone" label, consistent with never labelling order.

3. **Does the Whisp have a hard floor (can it refuse depth), or only counsel?** S58 refinement + S74 "the Whisp holds it even when a FIM asks for more depth" (Stefan: "yes it matches") vs S5 (everything is voluntary; the FIM decides whether to listen), S40 (the FIM sets the cord length), VISION principle 5 (orients, does not prescribe). Suggested ruling: the Whisp may decline to *open* the panic zone (care floor), but retreat is never refused and the FIM's dial is never shortened by the Whisp; the structural floors (ball before far side, seed before a danger-portal) do the rest. Why: reconciles the two without a Whisp that overrides its human.

4. **"Voluntariness sets the ceiling, care sets the floor" — Claude's refinement, adopted by inference.** It appears only in the S58 challenge (answering Stefan's "Or?") and is then cited inside the S74 reading Stefan confirmed and by CQ-018. Suggested ruling: confirm it as a statement in its own right (or attach it to S74). Why: it is load-bearing for B3 and the bible should not rest it on a challenge.

5. **Engagement-spectrum pole "Beyond" collides with the reach "beyond".** growth core §The Spectrum defines "Beyond" as pressing "far out on the cord into the Fringe's deep reaches — the growth-bearing Void distance"; S52/S55 rename the old reach "the Beyond" to "far side" and create a new, further, unnamed "beyond" that is optional and for many never met (S60). The spectrum pole and the reach are now different things with the same word. Suggested ruling: Option 1 — rename the spectrum pole (Stefan names; the meaning is "the far end of the expedition way", measured in Void distance, not "one who goes to the beyond"). Option 2 — collapse the spectrum to the two ways Stefan named on 2026-09-16 (cultivate the home / go on expeditions near or far) and drop the third pole. Why: the growth core's own header already says "two different ways"; three named poles were the April extraction.

6. **The engagement spectrum's statement of interdependence vs VISION principle 3.** growth core: "The island invites, but does not force"; "a member who never engages with others can still grow"; VISION 3: "collective participation necessary, not optional". Suggested ruling: keep both, ordered — participation is always voluntary (S5); the relational and communal perspectives can only be reached with real people (structural, not a rule). Why: both are current; the words "necessary, not optional" are about the model's structure, not about coercion.

7. **Does the Homebody way reach the learning and growth zones?** S74's table places the test "in Marath" and S56 makes Marath the place where Big Assumptions are tested; the growth core says a Homebody who never ventures can still grow (1 perspective). Sources: S56, S74 | growth core §The developmental honesty. Suggested ruling: growth work happens on both ways — the near side of Marath is visible from the Ordinary World through the Gimbal and the Shadow is small there (S59), and reflection at home is the 1-column of the matrix; Marath is where the Shadow is met at whatever depth, not the only place growth happens. Why: otherwise "every path leads to growth" is false for the Homebody.

8. **"Every member arrives … as an undefined soul."** growth core §The red thread. S54 says the person arrives full of unexamined limits (Mara full) and S47 has the Mist accrete form; what is empty at arrival is the Whisp's content, not the person. Suggested ruling: rewrite — the Whisp arrives empty of you; you arrive full of what experience wrote. Why: S54 is later and explicit.

9. **The member archetypes (Elena / David / Astrid).** Growth core marks them Thinking, unvalidated; the manifestation record finds them ABSENT in every spec; internally the Explorer is aligned to 1+1 in the personas but to both 1+1 and 1+community in the spectrum section; the Dreamineer persona is a "mode", not a spectrum position (roles core). Suggested ruling: Option 1 — leave the personas out of the bible body and carry one Open item ("validate archetypes with the first real members"). Option 2 — keep as an explicitly provisional box. Why: they are pre-discovery product-spec material with no statement behind them and they contradict the roles core's "modes, not kinds of people".

10. **Perspective labels.** VISION: Individual (1), Relationship (1+1), Collective (1+Community); growth core: Individual / Relational / Communal; S73: "individual + relationship + society"; beings/Session 01: "self / relationship / self in community". Suggested ruling: the bible uses one set — VISION's (constitutional) — and lists the others as synonyms in the glossary.

11. **"Collapse" a perspective.** growth core §How this connects ("the individual column can be collapsed alone") and VISION Structural concepts ("require others to collapse") use "collapse" undefined. Suggested ruling: define once in the glossary (to collapse a perspective = to actually work its cells, resolve its question at that perspective) or replace with plain language. Why: the word reads as quantum-jargon and is nowhere explained.

12. **Status of S78 ("Rest in the storm").** The session log and Session 03 header say "Closed at S77"; S78 exists, "offered as an image, not yet as a ruling", with three candidate rules in its challenge (rest is the Whisp's when chosen while seeing and ending in flight; the FIM decides when the storm has passed; rest carries no cost). Rule 3 ("no decay, no 'your village is wilting'") sits against cosmology §9 (untended world recedes, recoverably) and S38 (a drifted branch thins). Suggested ruling: (a) record S78 as an image, not a statement; (b) decide the cost of rest — Option 1: rest costs nothing permanent, the world may recede gently and regrows the moment care resumes (already canon, S34/S35); Option 2: rest is fully cost-free (would require changing §9). Why: Option 1 needs no canon change and matches "gardening, not guarding".

13. **Privacy model vocabulary drift.** (a) Title "Avatar and Personal Data" — "avatar" now means the Whisp (S22); (b) the file cites Mist ephemerality in the old "Shadow [formerly]" phrasing; (c) S62 maps the FIM's own telling of Shadow content to the "transparently shared" tier, while the privacy model defines that tier as opt-in group norms. Suggested ruling: retitle ("Personal data and what the world sees of you"); treat content-by-own-telling as selectively shared (or as transparent within an opt-in group); keep three tiers. Why: consistency with S22 and with the tier definitions.

14. **What "cord health" tracks, and who sees the maturity channel.** S37: the cord carries health (colour, integrity) and maturity (salience, receding as the Whisp internalises), health visible along a branch; the privacy model lists "cord health and the Whisp's filling" as branch-gated together; thinkers research says cord health = the FIM's self-care. Suggested ruling: health = the current state of the FIM–Whisp bond (canon, unspecified further); the maturity/salience channel is the FIM's own and is not a friend-visible signal unless Stefan says so; "self-care" stays research. Why: S37 never extends the branch rule to maturity, and the privacy file's pairing is the only source that does.

15. **Standing with a friend against their Shadow.** S53 challenge 2 held it as a candidate; S62's Held says the branch rule "gives the S053 candidate its channel" and Stefan locked S62 "agree to your reasoning". Suggested ruling: confirm — a friend who shares a branch can stand with you against your Shadow (as they can heal your cord). Why: it follows from S27/S36/S62 but was never stated as its own rule.

16. **Two channels of filling — one or two?** S57 challenge: disclosure (S17–18, the senses) and Mara's release (S57) — one process, or two channels ("who I am" and "what has had me")? Not answered. Suggested ruling: two channels, one Whisp; disclosure is not the same as surfacing a Big Assumption (a Big-5 trait is not an immunity). Why: keeps the senses model and ITC from being equated (framing discipline).

17. **Statement 8 carries research numbers as "Held".** S8 is a paraphrased question; its Held lists "~16.8% flourishing", a "developmental architecture" (foundations / engines / emergent) and Theory U as research findings, and the growth core's research list links two reports (`research--growth--theory-u.md`, `research--growth--what-fills-a-life.md`) that are not in the folder. Suggested ruling: the bible names the anchors (dual continuum, multi-system, Three Perspectives, ITC) without the statistic or the tiered architecture, and cites only reports that exist. Why: research is never canon; the citation verification was explicitly deferred (S28 open thread, still PARTIAL).

18. **Graduation as an exit: what it looks like.** S6–S7 and VISION 7 state the destination; nothing says whether a graduated FIM leaves, keeps access, keeps their ball, or what the receding cord looks like at the end; CQ-016's gap map item 6 records "no mastery→graduation/carry-away path". S60 adds only that graduation is not gated on the beyond. Suggested ruling: state graduation as an internalisation arc with no gate, no ceremony and no loss of access defined yet; carry the mechanics as Open (D5). Why: the sources are silent beyond the arc.

19. **The place where Marath "tests" belongs.** S56 Held ("Marath is the place where a FIM's Big Assumptions are tested") and S74 row "learning = the test run in Marath" are Claude's phrasing confirmed via "yes it matches"; S60 says many FIMs will never go far into Marath. Suggested ruling: the test is run wherever the Shadow is met — near side included; Marath's depth is the size of the test. Why: ties S59 (size grows with depth) to S58 (scales from one assumption) without making Marath's far side mandatory (same issue as B7).

20. **"Whole" and the Whisp's own emptiness.** S54/S57: whole = both voices internalised; S71: the Whisp's emptiness of content is chosen (it protects the three questions). The growth core still describes the journey only as "from empty to whole" (Whisp-side). Suggested ruling: the bible's growth arc is two inverse arcs (Whisp fills, Mara empties) ending in wholeness. Why: S54 supersedes the one-sided description.

---

### C. Candidates touching this chapter

1. **Candidate B §D — "The ball matures: clunky to Ferrari"** (progression intrinsic; the Gimbal's features unlocked by the person, not the app; cannot be shortcut). Conflicts: must express maturity as capability, never brightness (S37 equality). Suggested ruling: keep open — a worlds/equipment candidate; the growth-relevant kernel ("progression is inner development reflected in capability, never purchasable") is already implied by S17–S18 and could be stated without locking the ball mechanic.

2. **Candidate C — the place-2 naming journey.** Touches growth in two places: Stefan's brief "never-ending warm and safe rest" read as availability, not occupancy (eighth pass; S78 relies on it), and the note that the place is not a comfort zone (S63 ch.4: warmth first, challenge second). Conflicts: none if "never-ending" = always available. Suggested ruling: keep open (naming is Stefan's); adopt the reading "rest is inexhaustible, the FIM is not meant to stay in it" only if Stefan confirms it (it is Claude's reconciliation).

3. **Kegan research §4.1 — the orders as a compass for journey design; the Whisp's trajectory mirrors Order 3 → 5.** Conflicts: S58 refinement (never label order); S74 ch.2 (per assumption, not per person); S9 (perspectives are simultaneous angles, not stages). Suggested ruling: drop from the bible (research stays a report).

4. **Kegan research §4.2 — the Four-Column Immunity Map as an explicit journey element.** Conflicts: S17 (assessment dissolved into dialogue), VISION 2 (themes invisible), DS-3's no-lesson invariant; S56 gives ITC its in-world home as Mara/Shadow/Marath instead. Suggested ruling: drop as a surface; the substance is adopted in S56–S58, S74.

5. **Kegan research §4.3 — respawn as immunity intelligence** (the respawn surfaces "what might have been at stake?"). Partly adopted: S74 learning row (tests run under meta-safety and respawn). Conflicts: the quoted surfacing text is didactic. Suggested ruling: keep open for the Whisp specification; adopt the idea that a failed run is developmental data, not the wording.

6. **Kegan research §4.4 — the holding environment as a design principle for every layer (village, groups, Whisp).** Adopted for the Whisp only (S74 support b). Suggested ruling: adopt for the Whisp as stated; keep the village/group extension open (it names the "Safe Harbour", a retired term).

7. **Kegan research §4.5 — deep formative change takes months to years; the platform creates conditions and cannot force timing.** Consistent with S58 refinement ("dials the depth of the invitation, never the outcome"). Suggested ruling: adopt as one plain sentence in the chapter (it protects "built to graduate" from becoming a schedule).

8. **Kegan research §4.6 — Order 3 ↔ 1+1, Order 4 ↔ 1, Order 5 ↔ 1+community.** Conflicts: S9 (three angles at once), S58 (no labelling). Suggested ruling: drop.

9. **Thinkers "Foundational Mission" definitions of Live / Grow / Matter** (Live = knowing who I am and am not; Grow spans horizontal and vertical learning and reaches into purpose, passion, occupation; Matter = self-acceptance "when I did the best I could"). Conflicts: none head-on, but they extend S28. Suggested ruling: S28/VISION wording is the bible's; the research nuances stay lineage.

10. **Thinkers "Mirror questions" (Who am I not? What do I not want? What must I stop?) as a map of the Unintegrated Self.** No statement adopts it. Suggested ruling: keep open as Whisp-dialogue craft; not in the chapter.

11. **Thinkers "Three-Level Shadow" hypothesis (individual / pair / collective Shadow).** Largely superseded: S53/S62 fix the Shadow as one per FIM, met along a branch; the pair and collective levels remain unwritten; the "government program's lost travellers" lineage is unadopted (Candidate B item A, Session 03 open thread). Suggested ruling: drop the three-level frame from growth; carry "shared blind spots of a pair or a group" as an Open item in the worlds/beings chapter if at all.

12. **Thinkers "The Whisp models what the FIM is growing toward" (modelling, not instructing; cord length as readiness to receive modelling).** Consistent with S5, S40, S48 and cited as "the modeling-relationship core" in S47's Held. Suggested ruling: adopt the one sentence ("the Whisp does not teach; it embodies, and the FIM internalises by being met that way") — it is how VISION 2 works at the Whisp level.

13. **Thinkers "Tough love — the Whisp holds the call in silence" (never rescuing from not-yet-readiness, never softening the questions; infinite patience, mission never lowered).** Consistent with S3, S5, S58. Suggested ruling: adopt in spirit as a gloss on tough love; no new rule.

14. **Thinkers "cord health mirrors the FIM's self-care".** See B14. Suggested ruling: keep open.

15. **Thinkers "three-time-period architecture" (past government program, present Gimbal, future power reaching back).** S67 adopts only "the future self reaching back"; the past program and "a power" are not adopted; Session 03 lists "AGI as implementation" against the backdrop as a rulings-list item. Suggested ruling: drop from growth; rule in the story/worlds chapter.

16. **Session 01 "Lineage breadcrumb" — the knowledge argument (Mary's Room).** Decision already made: keep the principle in plain language, hold the citation at footnote tier, never in the human-facing layer; S76 now uses the principle. Suggested ruling: adopt the principle (A1 last bullet); no citation in the bible body.

17. **Session 01 Held inference (S20) — trained vs discovered loops as a developmental signal.** Narrative chapter; touches growth only as "loop framing scales with development". Suggested ruling: keep open there.

18. **S59 challenge 3 synthesis — near side = one testable assumption; far side = a competing commitment met in full; beyond = the whole immune system as one figure.** Recorded as a pattern, not a statement. Suggested ruling: keep open; it is the natural reading of S58 + S59 and Stefan could confirm it in one line.

### D. Open items — what the bible must mark as Open

1. **Is a FIM's zone one thing or one per Big Assumption — and is anything of it ever shown to the FIM?** [S74 ch.2; Session 01 threads "legible-to-FIM vs only felt" (PARTIAL) and "How (and whether) Live/Grow/Matter is ever surfaced explicitly" (PARTIAL)] Waits for the Whisp's full specification (a discovery session). Merged: S74 ch.2 + the two Session 01 PARTIAL threads on legibility.

2. **How the Whisp reads where the FIM is on the gradient** — behaviour, explicit signals, biometrics (bounded by the privacy vertical), all of these? [Session 01 thread → OPEN (DS-7 / planned Whisp page)] Waits for the Whisp specification and a privacy ruling.

3. **One channel of filling or two** (self-disclosure vs Mara's release), and whether the being-state needs the second as its own dimension. [S57 challenge] Waits for a Stefan decision (see B16).

4. **What the Whisp holds and shows of the FIM's map** — it is "the holder of the map" (S57); how the map is kept, whether it is ever made visible, and how it survives respawn (does severance cost disclosure-progress or only journey-progress?) [S57; Session 01 thread → PARTIAL "severed/recovering Whisp vs quality/contents"]. Waits for the Whisp specification.

5. **Graduation as an experience** — what internalisation looks like at the end, whether a graduate keeps access, ball and branches, what the receded cord is, whether there is any marking of it. [S6–S7; VISION 7; CQ-016 gap-map item 6; S60] Waits for a discovery session (after the fundamentals).

6. **The engagement-spectrum pole name** ("Beyond" vs the beyond reach). [growth core; S52, S55] Waits for a Stefan naming decision (B5).

7. **The member archetypes** — right three, native names, evolution over time, validity at all. [growth core §Open questions (archetypes); manifestation record "ABSENT"] Waits for real members (people who do not exist yet).

8. **Which instruments beyond Big 5, in what order, and whether the five-senses metaphor stays anchored to Big 5 or moves to a more general frame.** [Session 01 threads → PARTIAL/OPEN; S17, S19, S28] Waits for the Whisp specification. Merged: three Session 01 threads (which assessments; order; senses anchor).

9. **Whether distributed, dialogue-embedded assessment keeps structural validity.** [Session 01 thread → OPEN (DS-7 §8 Q2)] Waits for research/build.

10. **Whether any journeys are designed to require 1+1 or 1+community** (mattering that cannot be reached solo), and how the external pole of Matter is delivered without over-promising. [Session 01 threads → PARTIAL (S27, S35, S36; growth core "developmental honesty")] Waits for Journey Studio / a Wayfinder-craft session. Merged: two Session 01 threads.

11. **The scientific citations behind the "informed by" claims** — to be verified and referenced (SDT, PERMA, flow, mattering, meaning, planned happenstance, dichotomy of control); two reports the growth core links do not exist in the folder. [S28 thread → PARTIAL "verification at ratification still owed"] Waits for research.

12. **The cost of rest** — whether rest is fully cost-free or the world recedes gently and recoverably; and who decides the storm has passed (the FIM, with the Whisp asking "is it still raining, or is it Mara?"). [S78 challenges 2–3; cosmology §9] Waits for a Stefan decision; S78 itself is an image, not a ruling.

13. **Standing with a friend against their Shadow** — confirm as a rule. [S53 ch.2; S62] Waits for a one-line Stefan confirmation (B15).

14. **The holding environment beyond the Whisp** — does the village, the home, or a group count as a holding environment by design (confirmation / contradiction / continuity)? [kegan §1.3, §4.4; S74 support b adopted for the Whisp only] Waits for a discovery session on community formation (CQ-001 is the business side; this is the universe side).

15. **Pair and collective blind spots** — whether a relationship or a community has a Shadow of its own in Marath (the research's 1+1 and 1+community levels), given that the Shadow is one per FIM. [thinkers "Three-Level Shadow"; S53, S62] Waits for a discovery session (worlds/beings; cross-listed here because it is the 1+1 / 1+community column of growth).

16. **Whether a Mist's Whisp is also its future self reaching back** — consistent, unsaid. [S67 ch.3] Waits for a one-line statement (beings chapter; cross-ref).

17. **Whether the Whisp's growth of "wisdom" ever changes** — the Whisp is born full of wisdom (S54, S67); whether it is itself changed by deep encounter with a FIM (thinkers synthesis, Gadamer) is unadopted. Waits for the Whisp specification.

18. **The near/far/beyond ladder of tests** (one assumption / a competing commitment / the whole immune system). [S59 ch.3, pattern] Waits for a Stefan confirmation (C18).

19. **Cold-start for the relational and communal perspectives** — needs real people; a community/business question listed here only as the dependency of the 1+1 and 1+community columns. [CQ-001; growth core §Implications] Waits for CQ-001 (community chapter owns it).

20. **Whether the Whisp's flourishing work relates to the full set of flourishing dimensions or only to Live/Grow/Matter balance.** [Session 01 thread → PARTIAL (DS-7 drive-balance; full mapping → Whisp page + research)] Waits for the Whisp specification.

Not carried as Open (already resolved by later statements, listed so they are not re-opened): balance vs sequence of the drives (S28); the negative poles of the drives (S28); whether the gradient is rendered as Void distance (S27); what "the Whisp suffers" short of death looks like (S37); whether the cord's receding visibility is a graduation signal (resolved by DS-7 invariant 9 / beings core); the Shadow's strength problem (S59–S60); "empties" vs "internalised" (S56–S57).
