### E. Glossary terms for this chapter

- **season** — an authored unit of story, made of episodes, crafted by Tellers in Arc Studio [S13; roles core L1]. Cadence (four a year) is inherited platform design, not ruled [narrative core §Planned; B1].
- **episode** — the unit of story inside a season; the unit a whole-episode respawn replays [S13, S21].
- **journey** — a FIM's own path through the world, authored by Wayfinders in Journey Studio, alone / in a pair / in a group (= the Three Perspectives) [S13; roles core L1]. Nests seasons and episodes for respawn purposes: journey → season → episode → moment [S21 held].
- **arc** — the containing story that keeps holding a FIM through respawn ("the containing arc") [narrative core §Respawn]; also "a Teller's published Arc — a season or episode" [Candidate A #6, not locked].
- **A-plot / B-stories / sub-plots** — TV-craft terms in the narrative core's Overview, unratified [narrative core §Overview; B3].
- **signature journey vs charter trip** — bespoke to this FIM vs the same for everyone on the bus; disclosure breadth moves a journey along this continuum [S19].
- **route type** — Fixed / Hybrid / Traveler-Initiated / AI-Generative; a DS-3 registry, not universe canon [narrative core §Planned; B2]. Old name "Type 4 (AI-generative) journeys" [CQ-011].
- **content family** — Witness / Reflect / Decide / Act / Encounter / Rest; named in the planned journeys page only [narrative core §Planned; B2].
- **the Game** — a depth setting of journeys inside the one lived experience, not a product [ADR-U025 §5; VISION]. Retired: "the Game" as a third product entity (Unreal) [ADR-U025].
- **respawn** — the Whisp dying in a story and re-entering it; the FIM rewinds and retries [S19, S22]. **respawn topology** — the loop unit a story uses: event-local, round-bounded, day-bounded, episode-bounded [S20]. **loop texture** — the craft palette: combat practice, mystery-puzzle, constrained inquiry, reflective grief [S20].
- **the loop is the medium** — repetition is how the story works, not a safety net; what persists across the loop is part of what the story is about [S20; narrative core §Respawn].
- **home base** — a safe return point within the same journey/season/episode at which a FIM respawns [S21]; also the ball as "home base, seed-source, anchor-root" [S39] — one word, possibly two objects [B8].
- **episode-repeat** — respawn by replaying the whole episode [S21].
- **productive failure** — real felt stakes inside guaranteed reversibility; the condition under which deep learning happens [S19].
- **meta-safety** — the world contains threat; the experience of being in it does not; the FIM cannot be broken by the platform [S19].
- **felt stakes** — Jake does not want his Avatar hurt: caring about the projection gives the experience its weight without existential risk [S22].
- **the backdrop** — why the Whisp is here: the FIM's own future, whole self reaching back, because AI forks the future and growth into higher orders of mind is the lever [S66–S72]. Deep-bible tier; never told up front [S75–S76].
- **the dark branch** — the catastrophe future in the myth; the stake of the universe and the fundament of storytelling; never a whip [S66, S70, S73]. **the good future** — a world people want to belong to; a consequence the Whisp trusts in, never a target [S66, S68].
- **the conviction vs the myth** — the public real-world why (MANIFESTO) vs the discovered in-world story; available vs told [S76, S77].
- **deep bible / story bible / breadcrumbs / breadcrumb register** — proposed three knowledge tiers (full backdrop, held by canon keepers; rules and fixed points and what may be hinted, for Tellers and Wayfinders; hints in the world, for FIMs) plus a register of every hint and what it points to [S75, proposed; CQ-018; B9].
- **mythology arc vs free episode** — proposed: an arc that touches a fixed point or places a breadcrumb passes through the canon keepers; everything else is free, bound only by canon [S75; CQ-018 rec. 3].
- **canon keeper / showrunner** — proposed: a function inside Universe Studio that holds the deep bible; the founder is the first [S75; CQ-018 rec. 1].
- **fixed point** — proposed: a backdrop fact a mythology arc must not contradict (the Whisp's origin; the future at stake; what Marath's beyond is; the source of the Whisp's wisdom) [CQ-018 rec. 3].
- **the itch** — something that needs to be found interesting to gain curiosity; curiosity as a design principle [S70; S55; S76].
- **the zones** — comfort → fear → learning → growth, panic as the fence; the felt experience of the Mara-to-Whisp transfer [S73, S74]. Retired form: comfort → growth → panic [cosmology core §10; narrative core §Respawn].
- **the panic fence** — the boundary the Whisp never opens; a test too big to be safe strengthens the immunity [S74].
- **rest in the storm** — the butterfly image: rest is legitimate, protects what flies, and ends in flight; availability, not occupancy [S78, offered].
- **Teller** — the Dreamineer specialisation that authors seasons, episodes and named characters with arcs in Arc Studio; the particular, story scale [S29, S30; roles core]. **Wayfinder** — authors journeys in Journey Studio; the personal scale [S29, S30]. **Arc Studio / Journey Studio / Universe Studio** — the story and journey studios, and their parent and binding frame [S13, S14; ADR-U026]. Retired: "Arch Studio" (transcription drift) [S14]; Makers / Weavers / Skalds [roles core §Retired names]; "Weaver-AI collaboration tools" [CQ-011 old text].
- **NPC character layer** — the Teller-authored layer that makes an NPC a someone (name, history, wound, desire, arc); story-critical figures carry all three layers [S30; beings core].
- **the first hour / the first experience** — the founding narrative a newcomer first enters; the highest-risk gap; deferred until mechanics are firm [CQ-010; backlog]. Old name "Journey Zero" (the onboarding journey) [CQ-010].
- **Season Zero** — the Kickstarter as founding moment: backers arrive on the island, rewards are arrival rituals, campaign updates are the first episodes [community core]; not a season in the Arc Studio sense [B15].
- **immersive edutainment** — growth through being moved by stories that hold warmth and threat, life and death [S12; VISION].
- **the Hero's Journey** — the narrative framework named in VISION; not elaborated in discovery [VISION §What this is; B14].
- **"Alternative Reality"** — a logbook term for the reality-fiction blur; a design lineage (ARGs, analog horror), not canon [S23].
- **wound and cause** — every blight in the warm place has a cause in Marath; notice, cross, stop the cause, watch it heal [S32; cosmology core §3].
- **Retired names met in old story text:** "Shadow" for the anonymous entrant → the Mist (today's Shadow is Mara's form met in Marath) [S47, S53]; "Safe Harbour / The Other Side / Three Worlds" → the village / the Fringe (cosmology core "What this supersedes"); "the Beyond" → the far side, with a new unnamed beyond further out [S52, S55]; "affordance" → equipment [ADR-U025]; "FringeIsland" for place 2 → the universe's name, place 2's own name pending [S63–S65]; "AI Mentor" → the Whisp [project vocabulary; CQ-009 note].


# Chapter map — roles, governance, community and products

Chapters served: "Roles and governance" and "Community and the founding moment", plus the product/equipment facts a reader of those chapters needs. Sources read: `canon--roles--taxonomy.md`, `canon--community--kickstarter-season-zero.md`, `canon--cosmology--worlds-topology.md` §11 and "What this supersedes", `canon--beings--whisp-and-npcs.md` ("Where the creative roles went"), `canon--growth--how-growth-works.md` (spectrum, developmental honesty, archetypes), `canon--growth--privacy-model.md`, ADR-U025 / U026 / U028 / U029 / U031, VISION, MANIFESTO, PRINCIPLES-AI, discovery Part 1 (backlog, tracker), Part 2 (Candidates B, C), Part 3 (S10, S13–S16, S24, S29–S30, S39–S48, the 2026-06-05 product locks, S58, S63–S65, S66–S77 where they touch governance/Mists), the Session 01 patterns and open-thread tags, the CQ register (CQ-001..019), `record--universe-to-spec-manifestation.md`, and the research syntheses (Dunbar, the Three-Level Shadow) for section C.

Cite key: `[S<n>]` = discovery statement; `[roles L0]` etc. = `canon--roles--taxonomy.md` section; `[cosmology §11]`; `[community core]` = `canon--community--kickstarter-season-zero.md`; `[locks 2026-06-05]` = the "Product / ecosystem layer (design locks)" block in Session 01; `[ADR-U0nn]`; `[VISION]`, `[MANIFESTO]`, `[PRINCIPLES-AI]`; `[CQ-0nn]`.

---

### A. Settled — what the bible can state as true

#### A1. The shape of roles: an identity state plus three layers, not a ladder

- There is no role ladder. A person holds an **identity state** (L0) and positions in **three layers** at once: L1 FIM modes, L2 the enterprise-stewardship plane, with the studios and the Console as their surfaces. [roles "The shape: not a ladder"; S29]
- Roles are **modes a person occupies, not kinds of people**; the same person moves fluidly between the immersed and the authorial stance. [S29; roles L1; ADR-U026 §3]
- **One system, two faces:** every role is a set of permissions granted via groups (the universal group pattern, `has_permission`); fiction names and platform permissions are the same system seen from two sides, never two systems kept in sync. [S29; roles "The shape"; ADR-U028 decision drivers]
- Every participant is a **FIM**; you cannot build FringeIsland from truly outside it — to create *for* the world you must be a member *of* it. [S29 "[Locked:]"; roles L0]

#### A2. L0 — identity states: Mist, FIM, transcendence/metamorphosis

- **The Mist** is the anonymous entrant: a translucent, drifting becoming-figure in the **hyaline** state (present but not yet anchored, seen but incomplete). The Mist is the figure; hyaline is the condition. [S47; ADR-U031 "The identity"; roles L0]
- The Mist is first-class: it has its own Whisp and its own cord from the first moment and real presence in the shared near-side world. The Whisp is universal because it is the inner voice. [S39; S48; ADR-U031; cosmology §11]
- While a Mist, the cord is **unpaid-out** — at rest, near-zero length, the Whisp still "in the skull"; the Whisp is felt as the pull-toward-becoming, not met as a companion. [S48; ADR-U031 "Mechanics folded in"]
- The Mist **accretes** form as it answers the founding questions (the assessment dissolved into Whisp-led dialogue): eyes ("I can be met") → mouth and features ("I can respond") → firmer edges, reduced translucence ("I am nearly here") → condensation. [S47; ADR-U031]
- Accretion tracks **how far along**, never **which trait** was answered: stage is legible, content is private. Two Mists at the same stage look equally present regardless of who they are. [S47; ADR-U031 "Accretion tracks progress, never trait"; roles L0]
- A Mist can perceive and respond to FIMs live, gated by its accretion stage (pre-eyes: presence/motion; eyes: can be met; mouth: can exchange; definition: near-full participation). Every anonymous exchange is **live and unrecorded**. [S47; ADR-U031]
- Anonymous Mist presence is visible to FIMs and recognisable as a Mist. [S47]
- The Mist's access is **near-side only** (places 2 and 3, body-anchored). It has **no ball**, hence no anchor beyond the near side: no village, no private home, no deep Marath. This lock is **intrinsic, not a fence**: you do not enter the panic zone before you have a home to return to; the platform enforces the same rule by status, not by a permission fence. [S39; S45; cosmology §11; ADR-U031 stage 2; locks 2026-06-05 "Shadow is one status across surfaces"]
- A danger-portal needs a seed on its edge, and seeds bud from the home ball, so the seed rule hard-locks Mists out of deep place 3. [S40; cosmology §8]
- **FIM** — the base identity: the person with a Whisp, taking their equal place in the Tree. "Member" is the platform-technical synonym for FIM and is used for nothing else. FIM expands to "FringeIsland Member". [roles L0; S29; VISION "Structural concepts"]
- **Transcendence (metamorphosis)** is one single event, two faces: "transcendence" is the platform term, "metamorphosis" the lore term — the Mist condensing into the anchored, glowing ball in the Tree with full function (seeds, the far side). It fires **only when both** all founding questions are answered **and** consent is given. Consent is a precondition, never a separate earlier event. [S47; ADR-U031 "one event, not two"; roles L0]
- **Two entry paths, one gate.** Path 2: enter anonymously as a Mist, mature in-world, choose at the threshold — consent and completion coincide. Path 1: sign up at the door (consent first), walk in as a Mist that is already consented and persisted, mature as it answers — metamorphosis fires later, at completion, not at signup; in the interval the person is a consented, persisted FIM whose Mist has not yet condensed. [S47; ADR-U031]
- **What transcendence grants is the ball** — home base, seed-source, anchor-root; it opens the far side (village, private home) and deep place 3. It does not create the Whisp or the cord: it is the cord's **first paying-out** to length, enabled by the ball as anchor-root. [S39; S48; ADR-U031; cosmology §11]
- The cord is then **kept, not severed** (the umbilical inverted): FIM and Whisp are two-but-bound; cord length, set by the FIM, is developmental intimacy. [S48; S40]
- **Continuity is structural:** session experience transfers into the FIM with nothing restarting, and the migration is atomic (a last-moment joiner is never erased mid-migration). [S16; S46; ADR-U031 stage 4]
- **Signup buys memory, not voice.** Becoming a FIM does not unlock the ability to interact; it unlocks the ability for interaction to be **remembered** — branches, names, DMs, journal, forums, everything persistent or reciprocal. Durable cross-session memory is the FIM's, granted at transcendence. [S47; ADR-U031 incl. 2026-06-26 clarification]
- **Dissipation is "not yet," never death.** A Mist that drifts away returns to potential; on return it forms anew — a fresh Mist, not a resumed one. [S47; ADR-U031]

#### A3. The Mist's data and presence (privacy as mechanics)

- A Mist receives a server-issued **anonymous identity with no PII**. Server access is needed to perceive the shared near side at all, so client-only anonymity is not viable; the protection is **ephemerality, not refusing to store**. [S46; ADR-U031 stage 1]
- A Mist's own generated data (Whisp dialogue the most sensitive class) has no durability past session end: a short, configurable TTL after inactivity plus an explicit-erase path on close. Shared-world content the Mist merely read is out of scope. [S46; ADR-U031 stage 3]
- While anonymous, assessment answers are held **transiently** and **no trait-profile is computed** before consent; the profile crystallises only at metamorphosis. [S47; ADR-U031]
- Presence is **session-ephemeral and unlinkable**: no client-visible identifier ties a Mist across sessions; no interaction trail is persisted. [S47; ADR-U031]
- Device-local-only persistence (progress remembered on the person's own device, never server-side) is a permitted kindness; a server-side anonymous token is not adopted without a hard retention clock and no pre-consent inference. [S47; ADR-U031]
- A public FringeIsland entry (generic content) is reachable sessionless; a Mist is materialised lazily at the first act that enters or perceives the shared near-side world, not at page load. [ADR-U031 clarification 2026-06-26]

#### A4. L1 — FIM modes

- **Experiential (immersed) mode** is the default: the FIM journeying in the world, Whisp out on the cord. [roles L1; S29]
- **Authorial mode — the Dreamineer umbrella.** A Dreamineer is a FIM in their authorial, dream-it-into-being stance (from Disney's Imagineers; Imagine → Dream; theme park → a world for becoming). [S29; roles L1]
- Dreamineer authority is **not open to every FIM**: each specialisation is an authority granted via group and role permissions; entering a studio is a permission check. Becoming a Dreamineer is a FIM acquiring a role, not a change of being. [S29 "[Locked:]"; ADR-U026 §3]
- Four specialisations, each gating one studio: **Creator** → World Studio, hard side (terrain, water, rivers, mountains, sky, portals, 3D models); **Anthropologist** → World Studio, soft side (cultures, heritage, customs, peoples, countries); **Teller** → Arc Studio (stories as seasons and episodes; named characters with arcs); **Wayfinder** → Journey Studio (journeys alone / in pairs / in a group). [S29; S30; ADR-U026 §2–3; roles L1 table]
- The human appears at three scales, one per role: the Anthropologist authors the **typical** (collective), the Teller the **particular** (a named individual in a story), the Wayfinder the **personal** (the FIM's own path). Anthropologist and Teller are supply, not competition: the soil and the character grown from it. [S30; roles L1 table]
- **Support roles are per-group templates, not global tiers:** **Steward** leads and cares for a group; **Guide** facilitates a joint journey; **Participant** takes part; **Observer** watches. "Probably many more roles." [S29; roles L1 "Support roles"; ADR-U007 templates]
- Dreamineers run **dreaming sessions**. [PRINCIPLES-AI; S24 Held — see B14]

#### A5. L2 — the enterprise-stewardship plane

- L2 is genuinely separate from the FIM modes: it cares for the **continuation of the endeavour**, not a stance toward the fiction. The dividing line is "modes of being a FIM" vs "stewarding the project that sustains the universe" — not in-world vs out-of-world people. Its holders ideally also hold FIM identity. [S29; roles L2]
- **Universeers** care for: the constituency across the whole Universe; the ecosystem product portfolio; the community; economy; legal. [S29; roles L2; ADR-U028 §2]
- **The FringeIsland Council** cares for all major decisions concerning FringeIsland and its connection to partners. [S29; roles L2; ADR-U028 §2]
- **DeusEx** is the **human root-admin group of the running platform**: the people who administrate and maintain FringeIsland in the eyes of all users, the visible in-platform authority. Their permission set includes the authority of last resort. They act within the platform; they touch ecosystem development at releases and as a stakeholder, feeding observations of everyday platform life back to the developers — the link between life inside FringeIsland and the development of the ecosystem. [roles L2; ADR-U028 §2 (ratified 2026-06-10)]
- Root-admin authority is **role-based**: group membership is the container; the role's permission set is the authority; membership in DeusEx alone confers nothing. [ADR-U028 amendment 2026-06-12]
- Platform-scope authority reaches every group scope by design: governance by scope means the platform scope contains every group scope; the guard is that only system groups reach it and system-group membership is itself governed. [ADR-U028 amendment 2026-08-10, ruling A1]
- Anonymised aggregate member data may be explored only with explicit informed consent, responsible handling and clear member benefit — under enterprise stewardship (Universeers and Council, the Foundation as legal holder). Group Stewards cannot see members' private developmental data. [privacy model]

#### A6. Governance by scope and the Console

- **Governance splits by scope.** Community scope (a Steward moderating their own group, a Guide facilitating their journey) stays **woven in-place** in the FIM experience — no separate surface. Universe scope (economy, portfolio, legal, platform operations) happens on **the Console**, a distinct back-of-house surface. [locks 2026-06-05; ADR-U028 §1; roles L2]
- "The Console" is the working name; the fiction name is deferred. [ADR-U028 §1; roles L2]
- The Console is a **surface, not a new permission system** — one permission mechanism throughout. Whether it is a Hub-shell feature bundle or its own thin surface is a later decomposition decision; by equipment it leans comfortable-canvas + precision-input. [ADR-U028 consequences]
- Admin authority is structured by **scope + the universal group pattern**: community-scoped roles are the per-group templates; universe-scoped seats are the enterprise plane; no other ad-hoc admin roles are introduced. [ADR-U028 §3]
- Routing: content reporting/moderation in place; audit-log viewer and feature flags to the Console; a member leaving is self-service in-experience (not an admin act); a member buying is in-experience, economy management is on the Console. [locks 2026-06-05; ADR-U028 §4]
- **Gate by scope is a system law**, not an incident: the same rule splits governance (community vs universe) and World Studio authoring (your own home vs the shared world). [S44 Held; ADR-U026 §5; ADR-U028 decision drivers; Session 01 pattern "Gate by scope recurs" → CANON]

#### A7. The studios

- **Universe Studio** is the overarching parent and **binding frame**: it encapsulates World Studio, Arc Studio and Journey Studio and keeps the set coherent; coherence across worldbuilding, narrative and journeys is held at the Universe Studio level. It is not a fourth sibling. [S13; S14; ADR-U026 §1]
- **World Studio** — how the world looks and works, its places, lores, communities; two faces of one discipline: hard (Creator) and soft (Anthropologist). Terrain without culture is a stage set; culture without terrain is a history book. [S13; S30; ADR-U026 §2]
- **Arc Studio** — where Tellers craft narratives as seasons and episodes. "Arch Studio" was a transcription error. [S13; S14]
- **Journey Studio** — where Wayfinders craft journeys for FIMs to embark on alone, in pairs, or in a group/community; the three modes map onto the Three Perspectives (1 / 1+1 / 1+community). [S13; ADR-U026]
- Studios are a **role-gated authoring mode inside the one experience, not products**. [ADR-U025; ADR-U026 §3; locks 2026-06-05]
- Studios surface on both equipment profiles: World Studio has a capture-foot on sensors (scan the real world) and its deep edit on comfortable-canvas; Arc and Journey Studios lean canvas with light mobile review; no studio is bound to a device. Each journey declares its required equipment at authoring time. [ADR-U026 §4; ADR-U025 §3]
- **World Studio access tiers by scope:** furnishing your own private home — the personal-scope slice of World Studio — is open to **every FIM**; authoring the shared world is Dreamineer/Creator-gated. [S44; ADR-U026 §5; roles L1]
- The world is **continuously made** by contributing and collaborating FIMs, not pre-built; it is a living construction. [S13; cosmology §9 (gardening, not guarding)]
- An NPC is a world-inhabitant built in World Studio (body + culture) and promoted into Arc Studio's care (character layer) when narrative needs it. [S30]

#### A8. Why NPCs are not roles

- NPCs are authored, layered, depth-on-demand composites — body (Creator) + culture (Anthropologist) + character (Teller), layers added in proportion to the depth the role demands. They are world-inhabitants, not modes a person occupies, and have no Whisp. [S30; roles "NPCs are not roles"; beings core]

#### A9. Retired names and what replaced them

- **Visitor** → the Mist. [roles "Retired names"; ADR-U004 → U027 → U031]
- **Shadow (as the anonymous entrant)** → the Mist. "Shadow" is reassigned, not retired: it now names the hostile figure met in Marath — what Mara looks like when met there, one per FIM (see the beings chapter). Never blind-replace. [S47; ADR-U031; S53]
- **Member (as a per-group role)** → Participant. [roles "Retired names"]
- **Makers / Weavers / Skalds** → retired; the Dreamineer specialisations (Creator, Anthropologist, Teller, Wayfinder) are canonical. [roles "Retired names"; beings "Where the creative roles went"]
- **FringeIsland Studio** (planning name) → World Studio. [ADR-U026]
- **Arch Studio** → Arc Studio. [S14]
- **Three Worlds / Safe Harbour / The Other Side** → superseded by the topology: Safe Harbour is not a world but the village; the Other Side is the Fringe (two places). [cosmology "What this supersedes"]
- **The Member / Steward / Dreamineer / DeusEx ladder** and **Member / Steward / Dreamineer / Council** → not ladders; items from different layers stacked. [roles "Why the old ladders looked inconsistent"]
- **Affordance** → equipment (a naming decision, not a divergence). [ADR-U025 naming note]
- **The Game (as a product)** → a depth setting of journeys. [ADR-U025 §5]
- **"AI Mentor"** → the Whisp; a "Mentor/Elder" in CQ-009 means a *human* wisdom-sharing role, never the Whisp. [CQ-009 note 2026-06-22]

#### A10. Creating with AI: the constitutional principle, dreaming sessions, guard railing

- AI is an extension of human capability: it speeds production and widens the space of perspectives, while authorship, integrity, privacy and the originating creative intention remain wholly human. AI-first **and** human-first; when they conflict, humans first. [S13; S24; PRINCIPLES-AI]
- Sequence fixed: human intent first → AI expansion second → human selection third; the last say is authorship, the first thought is sacred (AI must not anchor the human). [S24; PRINCIPLES-AI]
- **Dreaming sessions** — facilitated ideation and the early steps of design thinking, run human-first before AI expands the field; AI may facilitate (ask, prompt, hold structure) but never generate content in this phase. [S24; PRINCIPLES-AI]
- **Guard railing** is bidirectional and always human-authored: rails that constrain AI (no acting without sign-off, no touching privacy, no unreviewed shipping, no pre-empting intent) and rails that protect the human driver (AI flags the author's blind spots against the author's own stated values). FringeIsland needs both because it deliberately includes a hostile pole, blurs reality and fiction, and pushes FIMs toward the growth zone. [S24; PRINCIPLES-AI]
- Nothing ships that a human did not choose to ship; the hand-placed feeling survives by construction. [S24 Held; PRINCIPLES-AI "the last say"]
- The Whisp is implemented by an AI, but in the lore the one speaking is the FIM's own future self; the AI is only the medium, and the Whisp borrows no authority from that backstory. [S67; S69]

#### A11. Products as equipment profiles

- There is **one lived experience** — being-in-FringeIsland (Mist + FIM) — over one shared core; products are shipped surfaces of it (a web app with a URL, a mobile app with a store listing), each with releases and a shell. [ADR-U025 §1; locks 2026-06-05]
- **The Hub** is the **canvas surface**: the canvas/tooling membrane (screen room, precision input, keyboard, file system, external plugins) — refinement and depth, at the desk. **The Gimbal** is the **senses surface**: the physical-world membrane (camera, LiDAR, GPS, microphone, AR display, hands-free, portability) — perception and capture, out in the world. [ADR-U025 §2; VISION "Ecosystem composition"]
- Hub and Gimbal name **equipment profiles, not devices**. Devices are points in equipment space: phone (high senses / low canvas), laptop (the reverse), tablet (the convergence point), AR glasses (max senses + immersion). A surface lights up on any device whose equipment matches; no device is an entity. [ADR-U025 §2]
- The surfaces are complementary, not clones: Gimbal-capture → Hub-refine (scan a real object on the phone, refine it on the canvas) is two ends of one workflow. [ADR-U025 §2; locks 2026-06-05]
- **Feature-grain equipment-keying:** every feature declares its required equipment from a small fixed coarse set (`sensors | comfortable-canvas | precision-input | none`) and appears wherever that equipment exists; chosen restriction is allowed but must be named by equipment, never by device or whim; extending the set (e.g. a future `immersion` key) is an explicit decision. [ADR-U025 §3]
- Products own only their shell (navigation, rendering, packaging, chrome); experience features belong to capabilities and light up wherever their equipment exists. "Is this a Hub or a Gimbal feature?" is retired; the question is "what equipment does it need?" [ADR-U025 §4]
- **The Game is depth, not a product**: a depth setting of journeys inside the one lived experience. Revisit trigger: a journey needs fidelity, an engine, or a play surface the mobile/web stack cannot render (AR glasses a strong candidate). [ADR-U025 §5; locks 2026-06-05]
- The Mist is **one status across surfaces, not a product**: gated by equipment (AR needs sensors) and by status (the ball gates the far side). [locks 2026-06-05; ADR-U031 stage 2]
- The glowing glass ball is visualised in the Gimbal and on the Hub as a two-zone gateway: press the inside → the private home; press the rim → the village. It mirrors across both profiles (a non-sensor feature). [S42]
- In the fiction, the Whisp is the faculty of perception, the Gimbal the device that opens the Shimmer to reveal the near side, and the Whisp together with the Gimbal is what lets a Mist perceive and pass. [S10; S39; S52; cosmology §2]
- "Product" remains the tier word — redefined, not retired. [ADR-U025 consequences]

#### A12. The referral model (one fact)

- Off-platform invitation is a **referral to the platform**: a FIM shares an invite link/code carrying a group as destination; the recipient arrives, transcends and consents to become a FIM, and only then receives a pending group invitation. Membership never precedes transcendence-and-consent; email-as-membership is retired. [CQ-017; ADR-U040]

#### A13. The canon-keeper function and the knowledge tiers (RECOMMENDED, not decided)

- What is settled: the backdrop is **never told up front**; the founders' conviction is public (MANIFESTO), the in-world myth is discovered; the secret is kept **for a while, not forever** — any FIM and any author can come to know it by growing into it; the answer exists before the first clue; the secret is kept at least in part from the Dreamineers who author seasons and episodes. [S75; S76; S77]
- What is recommended (Claude, recorded on Stefan's instruction; principles now, mechanics later): (1) canon-keeping is a **function inside Universe Studio**, not a new role — the founder is the first showrunner; later the Council seats the canon group and arbitrates but does not author; DeusEx is not the canon keeper. (2) Authors get the deep bible **by commission, not by rank or developmental level**; all Tellers and Wayfinders get the story bible; authoring access is never gated on a developmental order (the platform never labels a FIM's order, S58). (3) A **rule, not a list**: an arc is a mythology arc if it touches a fixed point or places a breadcrumb; the canon group keeps the fixed-point list. (4) The repository is not the vault; the secret is kept from the experience and the story bible, not from someone reading design files. [CQ-018 recommendation; S75 suggestion; S76 consequences]
- Three knowledge tiers as proposed: deep bible (canon keepers), story bible (Tellers and Wayfinders: rules, fixed points, what may be hinted but not what it means), breadcrumbs (FIMs, in the world), plus a breadcrumb register. [S75 (Claude's suggestion); backlog row "Narrative governance at scale"] — **mark as recommended**.

#### A14. Community: the founding moment (Extracted — never ratified; see B7–B9)

- The first public chapter of FringeIsland's story is told through a **Kickstarter campaign** — not primarily a fundraiser but a **founding moment**: a community-building event, a world-launch, an invitation. The campaign is **Season Zero**. [community core]
- Backers do not become backers; they **arrive**. Rewards are arrival rituals, not merchandise; campaign updates are the first episodes of the world; the campaign page is the first world-building document. [community core]
- The campaign speaks to two audiences: those who feel the pull of the three questions and want a world built around them, and **Founding Dreamineers** who recognise the invitation to build a world worth living in. [community core]
- The **founding Dreamineer cohort** — the people who self-select by backing at the world-builder tier — is the most valuable thing the campaign produces: the first Dreamineers, the seed of the world's authorial community, the people who shape FringeIsland before the wider world arrives. Dreamineer is an authorial mode, not a governing body. [community core]
- The campaign launches **after the full ecosystem vision is set**, not before; its design is its own dedicated session, approached with the same care as everything else. [community core; backlog row "Kickstarter / founding moment"]
- Campaign tiers, rewards, funding goal, video, pre-launch strategy: **not yet designed**. Campaign design is also blocked by the first-hour question. [community core "Open questions"; CQ-010 "Blocks"]

#### A15. Community ownership, contribution and the Foundation

- FringeIsland was not built to be sold; it belongs to the people who inhabit and create it, not to investors or shareholders. **The FringeIsland Foundation exists to hold that line permanently.** [MANIFESTO "Community ownership over corporate control"]
- Open contribution over closed gatekeeping: the world is built by many; the doors are open; the values are the only walls. [MANIFESTO]
- Member privacy over commercial opportunity; the platform never profits from what members entrust to it. [MANIFESTO; VISION principle 4]
- FringeIsland is a movement, not a product; the community is the reason the platform exists. [README "Community"; VISION "Ecosystem boundaries" — not a social network optimised for engagement]
- Humans and their stories outrank aesthetics and worldbuilding; constant change is the steady state. [S13; MANIFESTO (entered 2026-09-17)]
- The founders' "why now" (AI as the largest force of the time; inner growth has never mattered more) is stated in the MANIFESTO preamble as conviction only, never as lore. [S76; S77; MANIFESTO preamble]

#### A16. Three Perspectives and developmental interdependence

- The three founding questions each exist across **three perspectives**: Individual (1), Relationship (1+1), Collective (1+Community) — a nine-cell developmental surface. [VISION "Structural concepts"; growth core]
- **Developmental interdependence** is a non-negotiable principle: growth is structurally social; the relational and collective perspectives require others to collapse, making collective participation necessary, not optional. [VISION principle 3]
- The relational and communal perspectives structurally require **genuine human connection — not NPC interactions, not AI-generated activity, other people**. A member who never engages others can still grow, but the full arc opens only through connection; this is no judgment of the Homebody path. "The island invites, but does not force." [growth core "The developmental honesty"]
- Community and group features are not add-ons; they are the only path to collapsing the relational and communal perspectives; solo tools are as important as social features. [growth core "Implications"]
- Cooperative cord-healing and world-growing branches give the social layer a survival function — relationships keep your Whisp alive when stretched thin. [S27; S36; cosmology §7]
- The Dreamineer archetype illustrates the 1+Community perspective (growth through giving back) and is a mode, not a position on the engagement spectrum. [growth core archetypes — Thinking grade]

---

### B. Rulings needed — contradictions, stale text, ambiguities

1. **When does a person become a FIM on Path 1 — at consent (signup) or at transcendence (completion)?** S47's Statement: "become a FIM at the very start (consent first, then walk in as a Mist that is already a FIM…)". ADR-U031: "Becoming a FIM is the one moment data binds durably… Transcendence is the Mist → FIM transition" yet in Path 1 "persistence has begun" at signup and transcendence fires at completion; the person is "a consented, persisted FIM whose Mist has not yet condensed". Roles L0: "Transcendence — the Mist → FIM transition". *Suggested ruling:* Option 1 — FIM status (consent + persistence) is granted at signup on Path 1 and at the threshold on Path 2; transcendence/metamorphosis is the **ball-granting completion event** in both paths; a Path-1 FIM before completion is "a FIM still in the hyaline state". The bible should stop calling transcendence "the Mist → FIM transition" and call it "the moment the ball is granted (and, on Path 2, the moment consent is captured)". Why: it is the only reading under which S47's Statement, the ADR's Path-1 paragraph, and "signup buys memory" all hold at once.

2. **"Beyond" vocabulary in the roles core and ADR-U031 is stale.** Roles L0: "no ball, so no Beyond and no village"; ADR-U031 stage 2: "the Beyond (the village, deep place 3) is closed"; cosmology §11 column "Deep place 3". S52/S55 renamed the far reach the **far side** and added a further **beyond**; S50 named place 3 **Marath**. *Suggested ruling:* rewrite in the bible as "no ball, so no far side (no village, no private home) and no deep Marath"; reserve "beyond" for the further reach (Marath's beyond is unanchorable-by-candidate, S59–S60). No substance changes. Why: S55 is an explicit Stefan ruling that ratification must rename.

3. **The roles core's definition of "Shadow" lags Session 02.** Roles "Retired names": "'Shadow' now names the place-3 / sleep-paralysis menace" and cosmology §3 says its nature is open. S53: the Shadow is what Mara looks like when met in Marath, one per FIM; S62: it manifests only along a shared branch, form and force never content. *Suggested ruling:* the roles/glossary entry reads "the Shadow — Mara's form when met in Marath, one per FIM (beings chapter)". Why: S53/S62 are later Statements.

4. **"The island" and "FringeIsland" as place names in community text.** Community core: backers "arrive on the island"; growth core: "The island invites"; cosmology §6 "FringeIsland-the-village"; VISION: "the village — the safe-harbour commons". S63: "FringeIsland" is the universe/enterprise; S64: place 2 is a whole world, not an island; the village may or may not keep an island identity (naming-time detail). *Suggested ruling:* Option 1 — the bible says backers "arrive in FringeIsland" (the universe) and uses "the village" for the commons; "island" is used nowhere as a topological term until the place-2 name and the village's identity are decided. Option 2 — keep "arrive on the island" as campaign poetry with a note. Recommend Option 1 for a present-tense bible; why: S63/S64 supersede the extracted text.

5. **Can a backer "arrive" where the community text imagines?** The community core has backers arriving and the founding cohort shaping the world immediately. Under ADR-U031 a signed-up backer is a Path-1 FIM whose Mist has not condensed: no ball, no village, no far side until the founding questions are complete; Dreamineer authority is a separate permission grant (S29). *Suggested ruling:* state in the bible that arrival rituals happen on the near side and through the founding questions (the accretion is the arrival), and that founding Dreamineers receive their authority as a grant — how, open (D3). Why: the extracted text predates the Mist lifecycle and must not imply a shortcut past the consent-and-completion gate.

6. **"Founding Members" vs FIM.** Community core: "Founding Members" and "Founding Dreamineers". "Member" is reserved as the platform synonym for FIM (roles L0). *Suggested ruling:* "founding FIMs" (or "founding members" lower-case, glossed as FIMs) and "founding Dreamineers". Minor; why: vocabulary discipline.

7. **The community core is Extracted (April 2026) and never ratified** — everything in A14 carries "the founding vision says". *Suggested ruling:* the bible states A14 as the current intention with an explicit "not yet designed" box (D6), or Stefan ratifies it in the rulings session. Why: the brief and README both flag Extracted as unratified.

8. **CQ-009's source text no longer exists.** CQ-009: "VISION.md highlights 50+ as 'a particularly important role'". VISION 1.2 contains no such text; no Statement mentions the 50+ demographic. *Suggested ruling:* Option 1 — keep the question open in the bible as "is a human mentor/elder role wanted, and is Guide sufficient?" without the demographic claim; Option 2 — Stefan re-states the 50+ intent as a Statement; Option 3 — drop. Recommend Option 1; why: the bible cannot cite VISION for a sentence VISION does not contain.

9. **"Dreamineer Council" in the CQ register.** CQ-004 title "Dreamineer Council Governance Mechanics"; CQ-018 scope "Dreamineer Council". Canon: the Council is **the FringeIsland Council** (L2) and Dreamineer is an authorial mode, not a governing body (community core, roles L2). *Suggested ruling:* the bible names only the FringeIsland Council; CQ-004 is read as "how does the FringeIsland Council operate". Why: roles core ratified; the CQ headers are pre-canon.

10. **DeusEx and the Console have no discovery Statement.** DeusEx's formulation and "Participant" come from the Session B ratification (2026-06-10); the Console from the 2026-06-05 product locks (a design-lock block, not a numbered Statement). Both are bound by ADR-U028 (Stefan as decider). *Suggested ruling:* treat as settled (ADRs bind); the bible cites ADR-U028, not an S-number. Recorded so the spot-check does not look for a Statement that is not there.

11. **Universeers' care domains vs ADR-U028's list.** S29/roles: "the constituency across the whole Universe; the ecosystem product portfolio; the community; economy; legal". ADR-U028 §1 assigns "economy, portfolio, legal, platform operations" to the Console and §2 lists "constituency, portfolio, community, economy, legal". "Platform operations" is DeusEx's domain, not the Universeers'. *Suggested ruling:* the bible lists the Universeers' five domains from S29 and says platform operations belong to DeusEx; both use the Console. Why: consistent with roles L2.

12. **L2 must be FIMs?** S29: Universeers/Council "may (ideally do)" hold FIM identity — not locked; roles L2 "Ideally also FIMs (not locked)". DeusEx is not addressed at all. *Suggested ruling:* Stefan rules whether every L2 seat requires a FIM (consistent with "you cannot build FringeIsland from outside it") or not. Blocks a true sentence in the roles chapter; otherwise mark Open (D4).

13. **Dreaming sessions — Held vs Statement.** S24 Statement: "we can stage 'dreaming sessions' where we facilitate ideation". "Dreamineers run dreaming sessions" is the Held's vocabulary resonance, now constitutional in PRINCIPLES-AI. *Suggested ruling:* settled by PRINCIPLES-AI (constitutional wins); note only that who else may run one (Universeers? the Council?) is unstated.

14. **Home-creation as the Dreamineer on-ramp — offered, not locked.** S44 Held and ADR-U026 both say "offered, not locked". *Suggested ruling:* the bible states the personal-scope slice (settled) and marks the on-ramp as a candidate (C6).

15. **The Gimbal has two faces — platform profile and in-fiction instrument.** S10: "the Gimbal (i.e., the FringeIsland mobile app)"; S39: Whisp + Gimbal lets a Mist pass; S52: the Gimbal opens the Shimmer; ADR-U025: an equipment profile, not a device; Candidate B: an instrument with institutional origin. *Suggested ruling:* the bible states both faces under "one system, two faces" (S29): in the fiction the Gimbal is the instrument that opens the Shimmer; on the platform it is the senses surface on whatever device carries the senses; its origin story is Candidate B, not settled (C1).

16. **S16 "limited abilities" vs S47 "signup buys memory, not voice".** S16 has Mists with limited abilities; S47 says capacity to interact scales with accretion and only persistence waits for consent. Both true at different layers (near-side-only + accretion gating vs the memory layer). *Suggested ruling:* the bible states the limits precisely — reach (near side only), accretion (interaction grows with stage), and memory (nothing remembered) — and never says "limited abilities" unqualified.

17. **PRINCIPLES-AI and VISION cite retired paths/vocabulary.** PRINCIPLES-AI source line points at `thinking/universe-discovery/...` (a folder that no longer exists); VISION "The worlds" uses "a Beyond" and "safe-harbour commons" (pre-S55). Not contradictions of substance. *Suggested ruling:* editorial correction at the switch-over; the bible follows S55 vocabulary.

18. **The Console's fiction name; the Universeers' internal structure; the Council's relation to the Universeers.** All explicitly deferred (roles "Open threads"; CQ-004). No source disagrees; nothing blocks a true sentence except that the bible must say "deferred". → D4/D5.

19. **Community ownership assumes a Foundation that does not yet formally exist.** MANIFESTO and privacy model speak of the Foundation as the holder; CQ-006 (parked) says it is Wave 3 and not established. *Suggested ruling:* the bible states the Foundation as the intended permanent holder and marks "formal establishment: not yet" (a business item, one line). Why: a present-tense bible must not imply a legal entity that does not exist.

20. **"AI can produce NPC voices" and AI-driven NPCs.** S24 lists NPC voices as an AI speed use; CQ-013 (authoring mechanism) is parked. Not my chapter's ruling; noted because the AI-principles section will be read as answering it. *Suggested ruling:* the AI chapter section says AI may generate NPC voices/content under the rails; the authoring mechanism is Open (beings chapter).

---

### C. Candidates touching this chapter (Part 2 and unadopted research syntheses)

1. **Candidate B §A — the Gimbal as culmination of ancient knowledge; A(G)I as the implementation that closed the gap.** Conflicts: S66–S69 make the Whisp the FIM's own future self reaching back and put the outcome on humans, not AI; PRINCIPLES-AI forbids the AI-in-the-saviour-role framing; Session 03 open threads list this reconciliation explicitly. *Suggested ruling:* keep open, re-read against the backdrop; do not adopt as written.
2. **Candidate B §B — the key/instrument split (Gimbal = instrument with institutional origin, ball = irreducibly personal key; "they can build the instrument, they cannot use it").** Conflicts: S26/S39 give the ball three roles (home base, seed-source, anchor-root); "key" would be a fourth and must be stated as such; also implies institutions in the fiction (none in canon). *Suggested ruling:* keep open for a locking session; strong dramatic idea, not needed for the roles/products chapter.
3. **Candidate B §D — the ball matures (capability, not brightness).** Conflicts with S37 only if expressed as glow; otherwise compatible with "the Gimbal's features are unlocked by the person, not the app". *Suggested ruling:* keep open; if adopted, state "maturity as capability, never as brightness".
4. **Candidate B §H — the physical glass ball (2–3 cm, NFC-paired; community-developed; a possible Kickstarter or revenue line).** Paused by decision; the one fixed rule if revived: never required for access (a talisman, not a dongle). Touches the founding-moment chapter (a reward line). *Suggested ruling:* keep paused; the bible may list it under the Kickstarter's "not yet designed" box as a paused idea with its access rule.
5. **Candidate C — naming place 2 from the Whisp's voice.** Touches this chapter only through "FringeIsland = the universe and the enterprise" (S63, settled) and the retired "the FringeIsland place". *Suggested ruling:* keep open (Stefan names); the bible writes "place 2 (name pending)".
6. **Home-creation as the Dreamineer on-ramp** (S44 Held; ADR-U026 "offered, not locked"). No conflict. *Suggested ruling:* keep open; it answers part of D3 if adopted.
7. **Research synthesis — Dunbar's layered social capacity** (`research--growth--thinkers…` entry 22): sympathy group ~15 as a size for Dreamineer cohorts and small-group arcs; ~150 as the bound beyond which a community feature needs formal structure (moderation, roles); a community of thousands is not one tribe. Design input for community architecture; adopted in no Statement. *Suggested ruling:* keep as research; cite as an input to CQ-001 when the cold-start session runs.
8. **Research synthesis — the Three-Level Shadow (1 / 1+1 / 1+community)**: a collective Shadow as the community's shared suppressed material, "available for discovery by Dreamineers and FringeIsland anthropologists". Adopted in no Statement; S53/S62 give the Shadow a per-FIM, branch-scoped definition. *Suggested ruling:* keep open (beings chapter); the roles chapter does not use "anthropologist" in this sense.
9. **"A friend can stand with you against your Shadow"** (S53 challenge 2, held as candidate; S62 gives it the branch as channel). Community-layer mechanic, offered not locked. *Suggested ruling:* keep open for the beings/worlds chapters.

### D. Open items — what the bible must mark as Open

1. **How does a FIM acquire a Dreamineer authority — earned by journeying, granted by Stewards/Universeers, applied for, or via the home-creation on-ramp?** Sources: S29 (mechanism locked, route undefined); roles "Open threads"; S44 (on-ramp offered); Session 01 thread tags PARTIAL (2026-09-17 → CQ-002). Waits for: a Stefan decision or the community cold-start session; practically also for Dreamineers to exist. Merged: two Session 01 threads (the S29 acquisition thread and the earlier "how a FIM becomes a Dreamineer or Creator"), CQ-002 (recruitment before Dreamineer tools exist).
2. **What is the full support-role family beyond Steward / Guide / Participant / Observer, and what does each care for?** Sources: S29 ("probably many more roles"); roles "Open threads"; thread tag PARTIAL ("many more" open by design). Waits for: a discovery session, once groups have real use.
3. **Should the in-world role names be register-consistent (coined: Dreamineer, Teller, Wayfinder; plain: Creator, Anthropologist), or is the mix deliberate?** Sources: S29 naming-register note; roles "Open threads"; thread tag OPEN (deferred per S29). Waits for: a Stefan decision (naming).
4. **Must Universeers, Council members and DeusEx also be FIMs?** Sources: S29 ("ideally"); roles L2 "not locked"; thread tag OPEN (→ CQ-004). Waits for: a Stefan decision. See B12.
5. **How is the enterprise plane structured — are the Universeers' five care domains sub-roles, committees or one role; how does the Council relate to them; how does the Council operate (voting, consensus, rotating seats) and what tooling does it need?** Sources: roles "Open threads"; CQ-004; thread tag OPEN (CQ-004; PC-4 "admin-role granularity"). Waits for: a Stefan decision and people who do not exist yet. Merged: two Session 01 threads + CQ-004.
6. **What is the Console's fiction name?** Sources: locks 2026-06-05; ADR-U028 §1; roles "Open threads". Waits for: a Stefan decision (naming).
7. **Who keeps canon after the founder; how an author receives the deep bible; which arcs are mythology arcs; where the deep bible lives — principles recommended, nothing decided.** Sources: S75–S76; CQ-018; backlog row "Narrative governance at scale"; Session 03 open thread "Governance of the mythology". Waits for: a Stefan decision on the four principles (recommended in CQ-018), then mechanics once CQ-004 is settled and Dreamineers exist; the reveal design itself waits on the fundamentals (2026-06-14 rule). Repository tiering (CQ-019) is technical and is not a universe question.
8. **How are dreaming sessions actually run — solo, group, FIM-facing; where in Universe Studio; and where exactly does AI-as-facilitator end and AI-as-generator begin (can AI's questions anchor)?** Sources: S24; thread tags PARTIAL (→ studio DESCRIPTIONs, Eid; the anchoring nuance still flagged). Waits for: build (the studios). Merged: two Session 01 threads.
9. **Guard rails — at what levels (ecosystem-wide, per story, per Dreamineer), how versioned and enforced, how surfaced without nannying, and are the rails themselves authored by the stage model?** Sources: S24; thread tags PARTIAL / OPEN ("PRINCIPLES-AI does not say"). Waits for: a Stefan decision plus studio design. Merged: three Session 01 threads.
10. **How is AI used inside each studio, and how are individual contributions protected technically and culturally (attribution, authorship, withdrawal)?** Sources: S13; S24; thread tags PARTIAL (→ studio DESCRIPTIONs; CQ-007 licensing). Waits for: studio design (build); CQ-007 is a business/legal item and is not a universe question.
11. **The inter-studio collaboration protocol: who decides how much depth an NPC gets and when; does a Teller reach back into World Studio or author depth in Arc Studio?** Sources: S30 (deferred); thread tag OPEN (planned NPC page; the studios). Waits for: build (the studios) and the NPC page. (Shared with the beings chapter.)
12. **How do Dreamineers manifest within the narrative rather than behind it?** Source: beings core "Where the creative roles went" (open design thread). Waits for: a discovery session (narrative).
13. **Whether a single Dreamineer can author a whole coherent world (the Akerman bar) or worlds are inherently collaborative — and how the quality bar is met collaboratively; how "creation as discovery" is designed for inside Universe Studio.** Sources: S25; S35; S13; thread tags PARTIAL / OPEN (→ the studios, Eid). Waits for: build. Merged: two threads.
14. **Community cold-start: how are enough human relationships seeded for the 1+1 and 1+community perspectives to work; what is the minimum viable community; how are Dreamineers recruited before Dreamineer tools exist; how does content get made before there is a community to make it?** Sources: CQ-001, CQ-002, CQ-003; backlog row "Community formation / cold-start"; growth core "Implications"; manifestation record gap 3; Session 01 pattern "Collective intelligence is part of the form" (→ OPEN). Waits for: a discovery session; people who do not exist yet. Merged: three CQs + the backlog row + the pattern tag.
15. **The Kickstarter / founding moment: tiers, rewards, funding goal, video, pre-launch, the arrival rituals, Season Zero as the first episodes.** Sources: community core "Open questions"; backlog row "Kickstarter / founding moment"; tracker "not yet graduated"; CQ-010 (campaign design blocked by the first hour); Candidate B §H (physical ball, paused). Waits for: the full ecosystem vision set (community core's own rule), the first hour (CQ-010), the universe-mechanics fundamentals, and a dedicated session. The Foundation as legal recipient (CQ-006) is a business item, one line.
16. **Is a human mentor/elder role wanted (the 50+ question), or is Guide sufficient?** Source: CQ-009 (parked; note that "Mentor" never means the Whisp). Waits for: a Stefan decision; the source text in VISION is gone (B8).
17. **What can a Mist see and do at product level — which pages/content, the taster journey, the garden glimpse; and how is a Mist perceived in-world by FIMs and NPCs?** Sources: CQ-014 (narrowed); thread tag PARTIAL ("in-world perception → universe-mechanics fundamentals"). Waits for: the fundamentals session and a feature spec. Merged: CQ-014 + one Session 01 thread.
18. **Does a Mist's Whisp also come from the FIM's own future self?** Source: S67 challenge 3 ("consistent, but worth saying"); unanswered. Waits for: a one-line Stefan confirmation.
19. **Can the Whisp be perceived without the Gimbal (does internalisation include perception), and how is the Whisp represented on the Hub before AR?** Sources: Session 01 thread tag PARTIAL (ADR-U025 + ADR-U029; the remainder is CQ-012). Waits for: the Whisp's full specification and the Gimbal (Eid+). (Shared with the beings chapter.)
20. **Is the Console a Hub-shell feature bundle or its own thin surface?** Source: ADR-U028 consequences. Waits for: build (decomposition when Console features are specified). Technical; the bible needs one line at most.

22. **The physical glass ball** — paused by decision (Candidate B §H); if ever revived, never required for access. Waits for: a trigger (Kickstarter design).

Excluded as business/technical, not universe: CQ-005 (monetisation timing), CQ-006 (Foundation establishment — one line under community ownership only), CQ-007 (licensing), CQ-008 (physical products), CQ-011 (AI feasibility), CQ-015 (resolved), CQ-016 (Hub trajectory — spec framing), CQ-019 (repository set-up).

### E. Glossary terms for this chapter

- **FIM** — FringeIsland Member: the base identity, a person with a Whisp, taking their equal place in the Tree. [roles L0; VISION]
- **Member** — the platform-technical synonym for FIM; used for nothing else. [roles L0]
- **Mist** — the anonymous entrant: a translucent, drifting becoming-figure that accretes form as it answers the founding questions; near-side only; ephemeral, unlinkable; has its own Whisp and cord from the start. [S47; ADR-U031]
- **Hyaline (state)** — the translucent, unanchored, not-yet-committed condition a Mist is in; the Mist is the figure, hyaline the condition. [S47; ADR-U031]
- **Accretion** — the Mist filling in (eyes → mouth → definition → condensation) as the founding questions are answered; tracks progress, never trait. [S47]
- **Transcendence** — the platform term for the one event that grants persistence, consent and the ball. [ADR-U031]
- **Metamorphosis** — the lore term for the same event: the Mist condensing into its glowing glass ball in the Tree; the cord's first paying-out. [S47; S48]
- **The ball** — the glowing glass ball granted at transcendence: home base, seed-source, anchor-root; a two-zone gateway (inside → home, rim → village). [S39; S42]
- **Dreamineer** — a FIM in their authorial, dream-it-into-being mode; permission-gated; four specialisations. From Disney's Imagineers. [S29]
- **Creator** — Dreamineer specialisation: the hard side of World Studio (physical substrate). [S29; S30]
- **Anthropologist** — Dreamineer specialisation: the soft side of World Studio (cultural substrate; the typical). [S29; S30]
- **Teller** — Dreamineer specialisation: Arc Studio (seasons, episodes, named characters; the particular). [S29; S30]
- **Wayfinder** — Dreamineer specialisation: Journey Studio (journeys alone / pairs / group; the personal). [S29; S30]
- **Steward** — per-group support role: leads and cares for a group. [roles L1]
- **Guide** — per-group support role: facilitates a joint journey. [roles L1]
- **Participant** — per-group support role: takes part (renamed from the group role "Member"). [roles L1]
- **Observer** — per-group support role: watches. [roles L1]
- **Universeers** — L2 seats caring for the constituency, the product portfolio, the community, economy, legal. [S29]
- **The FringeIsland Council** — L2 seat for major decisions and partner relations. [S29]
- **DeusEx** — the human root-admin group of the running platform; holds the authority of last resort; the link between platform life and ecosystem development. [ADR-U028]
- **The Console** — working name for the back-of-house surface of universe-scoped governance; fiction name deferred. [ADR-U028]
- **Governance by scope** — community care in place, universe governance on the Console; the same law tiers World Studio access. [ADR-U028; ADR-U026]
- **Universe Studio** — the parent and binding frame over World, Arc and Journey Studios. [S13–14; ADR-U026]
- **World Studio / Arc Studio / Journey Studio** — the three role-gated authoring modes (world, story, journeys). [ADR-U026]
- **Dreaming session** — a human-first facilitated ideation ritual before AI expands the field; AI facilitates, never generates. [S24; PRINCIPLES-AI]
- **Guard railing** — bidirectional, human-authored rails: constraining AI and protecting the human driver. [S24; PRINCIPLES-AI]
- **Equipment** — a device's capabilities (senses, canvas, precision input); the word "affordance" is retired. [ADR-U025]
- **Equipment profile** — a surface of the one experience defined by the equipment it needs, not by a device. [ADR-U025]
- **The Hub** — the canvas surface (refinement and depth, at the desk). [ADR-U025]
- **The Gimbal** — the senses surface (perception and capture, out in the world); in the fiction, the instrument that opens the Shimmer. [ADR-U025; S10; S52]
- **The Game** — not a product: a depth setting of journeys. [ADR-U025]
- **Referral** — a FIM-shared invite to the platform; membership follows transcendence-and-consent, never precedes it. [ADR-U040]
- **Season Zero** — the Kickstarter campaign as the founding moment and first chapter. [community core]
- **Founding Dreamineer cohort** — the world-builder-tier backers; the seed of the authorial community. [community core]
- **The FringeIsland Foundation** — the intended permanent holder of community ownership; not yet formally established. [MANIFESTO; CQ-006]
- **Three Perspectives** — Individual (1), Relationship (1+1), Collective (1+Community). [VISION]
- **Developmental interdependence** — growth is structurally social; the relational and collective perspectives need other people. [VISION principle 3]
- **Canon keeper (recommended)** — a function inside Universe Studio holding the deep bible; the founder first. [CQ-018]
- **Deep bible / story bible / breadcrumbs (recommended)** — the three knowledge tiers of the backdrop. [S75; CQ-018]

Retired names → replacement: Visitor → Mist; Shadow (entrant) → Mist (Shadow now = Mara's form in Marath); Member (group role) → Participant; Makers / Weavers / Skalds → the Dreamineer specialisations; FringeIsland Studio → World Studio; Arch Studio → Arc Studio; Three Worlds / Safe Harbour / The Other Side → Ordinary World / the village / the Fringe (two places); affordance → equipment; the Game (product) → journey depth; AI Mentor → the Whisp; the Member/Steward/Dreamineer/DeusEx ladder → the L0/L1/L2 model; "Dreamineer Council" → the FringeIsland Council; "the island" / "the FringeIsland place" for place 2 → place 2 (name pending; FringeIsland = the universe).


# The open-items sweep

*Phase 2 inventory for the FringeIsland Universe Bible. Read-only; nothing here is a ruling.*
*Swept 2026-09-24 against `docs/fringeisland-thinking/` (the discovery file, the seven canon cores, the CQ register, the manifestation record) and the Phase 2 brief.*

**Cite keys used below**
- `T<line>` — a Session 01 reconciliation tag, by its line number in `discovery--the-universe-in-the-making.md` (Patterns: L2208–L2283; Open threads: L2330–L2457). Every tag is quoted in Appendix 1 with the statement it sits under.
- `S<n> ch<k>` — challenge k under Statement n (Sessions 02–03). Every challenge is dispositioned in Appendix 2.
- `S03-OT<k>` — the k-th bullet of Session 03's "Open threads".
- `BL<k>` — the k-th row of the Part 1 discovery backlog; `SB-A/B` — the sounding-board rows.
- `CB-<letter>` — Candidate B item; `CA` — Candidate A; `CC` — Candidate C.
- `CQ-nnn` — the register; `REC-gap<k>` — the manifestation record's prioritised gap k; `<core> planned/open` — a canon core's "Planned — not yet written" page or its open-threads section.

### B. Session 01 → OPEN / → PARTIAL tags that are not universe questions — propose dropping from the bible's open boxes

27 of the 102 tags. Grouped by why they are not open questions.

**(i) Facts or design intents without a home — state them in the bible as settled, not as open (5)**
- T2208 (S10) "The hidden-layer feeling matters" — an experiential signature; state it under worlds (near side) / story tone.
- T2227 (S17–18) "The motivation to engage is intrinsic and sensory" — a design intent; state it under beings (the senses).
- T2230 (S19) "Signature journey vs charter trip" — Stefan's principle; state it in the journeys section (O-055 is the page, not the fact).
- T2246 (S23) "Borrow the mystery aesthetic, not the exclusion" — an experience principle; state it under story.
- T2283 (S30) "The hard/soft (outer/inner) pattern recurs" — an observed structural signature; state once in Start here or drop; not a question.

**(ii) Design-lineage records — belong in a lineage note or the research register, not an open box (5)**
- T2243 (S23) ARG / analog-horror lineage; T2244 (S23) blur runs both ways; T2245 (S23) collective intelligence (its only open residue is CQ-001/003 → O-074); T2252 (S25) Akerman / Stalenhag as quality references; T2253 (S23, S25) two reference families. (T2254 / T2416 — whether the Nordic lineage is *deliberate* — is a real question and stays as O-066.)

**(iii) Studio, AI-principles and spec obligations — owned by PRINCIPLES-AI, the studio DESCRIPTIONs or a DS build, not by the universe (15)**
- T2348 (S24) how AI is used inside each studio; T2409 (S24, S29) how dreaming sessions are run and where; T2410 (S24) facilitator-vs-generator line; T2411 (S24) who authors guard rails, levels, versioning; T2412 (S24) how AI-catches-human rails are surfaced; T2413 (S24) whether rails are authored by the stage model — all PRINCIPLES-AI / studio DESCRIPTION questions.
- T2349 (S13, S24) attribution / withdrawal mechanisms → CQ-007 (legal, stays in register).
- T2376 (S18) sensory layering as a craft discipline inside Universe Studio; T2417 (S25, S35, S13) how the single-author quality bar is met collaboratively; T2419 (S25) designing for "creation as discovery" — studio craft.
- T2385 (S20, S21, S27) which respawn topologies are *implemented* — "the built set → Eid", a build decision (the plural set is canon).
- T2424 (S26, S33, S34, S35) granular home permissions "in practice" — the DS-1 build.
- T2406 (S19, S24) the reality-fiction blur line; T2414 (S24) safety bounds on real-world integration; T2438 (S24, S27) bodily mobility vs real-world safety — "a design discipline" under PRINCIPLES-AI guard rails and the Gimbal DESCRIPTION; the bible states the principle (meta-safety, guard rails) and owns no question.

**(iv) Ratification / process obligations (2)**
- T2446 (S28) verify the scientific citations behind "informed by" — owed at the growth chapter's writing, not an open question about the universe.
- T2447 (S28) the Mary's-Room lineage breadcrumb — "parked by decision, unchanged"; footnote-tier at most; keep in the research register.
