# Glossary draft (assembled from the chapter maps; duplicates left for the glossary chapter to merge)

## From worlds
- **FringeIsland** — the universe: everything past the Shimmer, both worlds and all three reaches; also the enterprise that makes it. [S63]
- **The Ordinary World** — daily life, unmodified; where the FIM's body always is; the substrate the near sides overlay. [opening frame, S27, S49]
- **The Shimmer** — the one membrane between the Ordinary World and both worlds; a line, not a band; perceived through the Gimbal by the Whisp. [S26, S31, S49, S52]
- **The Fringe** — the inhabited band past the Shimmer: the two worlds and their reaches (see B.2). [S26]
- **Place 2 (name pending)** — the warm, welcoming world; resonates with the Whisp; tendable, woundable; a whole world, not an island. [S31, S51, S63, S64]
- **Marath** — place 3: the hostile, dystopian world; the manifestation of Mara; the counterpart of place 2. [S50, S51]
- **Mara** — the negative inner voice, obeyed unseen; empties as the Whisp fills. [S51, S54, S57]
- **The Shadow** — what Mara looks like when met in Marath; one per FIM; grows with depth; visible to others only along a shared branch, form and force not content. [S53, S59, S62]
- **Near side** — the reach of either world overlaid on Ordinary-World coordinates, seen through the Shimmer; body-anchored; Mist-open. [S45, S52]
- **Far side** — the reach of either world away from the Ordinary World, with no earthly coordinates; ball- or seed-anchored; FIM-only. Formerly "the Beyond". [S52, S55]
- **The beyond** (name pending) — the reach beyond the far side; unknown; Marath's beyond is Mara at full strength; optional; possibly unanchorable. [S52, S55, S59, S60]
- **The near-side coupling** — the same coordinates seen two ways; every wound in place 2 has a cause in Marath. [S32]
- **The Void** — the axis of separation: wherever the Whisp is once it leaves the FIM, along the whole cord; also the primordial black nothingness Marath expresses and place 2 is revived from. [S27, S34, S35]
- **The cord** — the FIM–Whisp lifeline through the Void; cut, the Whisp suffers or dies, never the FIM; kept from the Mist's first moment, first paid out at metamorphosis. [S27, S40, S48]
- **The dial** — the cord's length, set by the FIM: pay out to venture, reel in to retreat; developmental intimacy. [S40, S48]
- **Void distance** — how far the Whisp has ventured on the cord; the distance that carries risk and growth (as against bodily distance, which is safe). [S27]
- **Cord health / cord maturity** — two channels: health is colour and integrity; maturity is salience (vivid early, receding). [S37]
- **The branch** — the FIM-to-FIM bond; the material of the Tree's crown; the channel for cord-healing and for seeing a friend's cord or Shadow. [S36, S38, S62]
- **The crown** — the woven branches between balls; own branches legible, the rest ambient. [S35, S38]
- **Anchoring** — fastening the cord to objects along a route, like a climber's protection. [S27]
- **The anchor ladder** — body (near side) → ball (far side of place 2) → seeds (into Marath); anchoring is the gate between reaches. [S45]
- **Seed** — an anchor object budded from the FIM's ball; at once protection and a patch of place-2 life reclaimed from dead ground. [S40]
- **Seed-to-pass** — a danger-portal needs a seed on its edge; the threshold seed is anchor-zero and the way home. [S40]
- **Danger-portal / field portal** — a portal from the Ordinary World straight into Marath's depths, threading the cord out. [S40]
- **The ball (the glowing glass ball)** — one per FIM, granted at metamorphosis; hangs in the Tree; glows equal; the only inviolable thing; home base, seed-source, anchor-root; the two-zone gateway (inside → home, rim → village). [S26, S34, S37, S39, S42, S47]
- **The Tree** — the great tree in the village hung with the balls; the portal between the commons and all private places; the social graph is the Tree. [S26, S38]
- **The village** (working name) — the safe-harbour commons on the far side of place 2, a sea-village; FIM-only. [S26, S41, S45, S55]
- **The private home** — a FIM's self-chosen representation of where they feel safest; default-locked; selectively shareable by part; furnished from World Studio; evolves. [S43, S44]
- **Gardening, not guarding** — the rule for the tendable world: tending feels like a beloved garden; nothing is permanently destroyed, only not-yet-grown or receded. [S34]
- **World-genesis** — balls rise from the black ground, bonds weave the crown, the tree brings the ground to life → village → island → place 2; place 2 is revived Marath; the world is inherited. [S35]
- **Severance (two-tier)** — anchored: fall back to the last anchor; unanchored overreach: start from the very beginning; recovery is reunion. [S27]
- **Dead cord / stuck cord** — a closing portal with a destroyed seed kills the cord; with a living seed it freezes the length (reopenable, rescuable along a branch). [S40]
- **The zones** — comfort → fear → learning → growth, panic as the fence the Whisp never opens; the felt experience of the Mara-to-Whisp transfer; bands on the Void-distance gradient. [S73, S74]
- **The breath-rhythm** — venture out for challenge, return home for safety. [S26, cosmology §10]
- **Meta-safety** — the human is never the thing at risk; stakes live in the Whisp. [S19, S22]
- **The Gimbal** — the senses surface (camera, GPS, AR…): the equipment profile that opens the Shimmer. [S10, S52, ADR-U025]
- **The Hub** — the canvas surface; both profiles show the ball. [S42, ADR-U025]
- **Equipment** — the canon word for device capabilities (never "affordance"). [ADR-U025]
- **The Mist** — the anonymous becoming-figure: own Whisp and cord, near side only, no ball. [S47, ADR-U031]
- **Metamorphosis / transcendence** — one event: the Mist condenses into its ball in the Tree when the questions are complete and consent is given; the cord first pays out. [S47, S48, ADR-U031]

**Retired names and what replaced them**
- Three Worlds (Ordinary World / Safe Harbour / The Other Side) → Ordinary World / the Shimmer / the two worlds behind it. [cosmology "What this supersedes"]
- Safe Harbour (a world) → the village (a commons on place 2's far side). [S33, S41]
- The Other Side → the Fringe: two worlds, each with three reaches. [S31, S52]
- The Void "between worlds" / its "three dimensions" → the Void as axis. [S27; growth core note]
- "The Beyond" (capitalised, the untethered reach) → the far side; "the beyond" is now the reach past it. [S55]
- "Overlay / overlaid register" → near side; "beyond register" → far side. [S26]
- "FringeIsland-the-island", "the FringeIsland place", "Place 2 is FringeIsland" (S50) → place 2 (name pending); FringeIsland is the universe. [S63, S64]
- "Space 3", "place 3 (unnamed)" → Marath. [S50]
- "Shadow" as the anonymous entrant; "Visitor" → the Mist. [S47, ADR-U031]
- "Shadow-side" (Candidate A) → the near-side coupling; never used. [S32]
- "Home portal" (a separate portal beside the FIM) → the ball's inside. [S42]
- "Safe harbor = comfort zone; distance from the island = growth" → Void distance on the cord. [S27]
- "Comfort → growth → panic" (three steps) → comfort → fear → learning → growth, panic as the fence. [S74]
- "AI Mentor" → the Whisp; "affordance" → equipment. [brief vocabulary; ADR-U025]
- "Beyond" as an engagement-spectrum pole (growth core) → rename or re-label (B.1).

## From beings
- **the Whisp** — each person's own inner dialogue oriented toward growth and compassion; the person's own future, whole self reaching back; also the avatar through which the FIM walks the Fringe on the cord. Empty of content, full of wisdom. [S1, S4, S22, S54, S67]
- **Mara** — the negative inner voice, obeyed unknowingly; the person's immune system in the Immunity-to-Change sense; full of content at the start, its authority empties as the Whisp fills. Manifested as Marath. [S51, S54, S56]
- **the Shadow** — what Mara looks like when met in Marath; one per FIM; grows with depth into Marath; visible to others only along a shared branch, as form and force, never content. [S53, S59, S62]
- **Mist** — the anonymous entrant: a translucent, drifting becoming-figure in the hyaline state, with its own Whisp and unpaid-out cord; near-side only; ephemeral and unlinkable. [S47, S48; ADR-U031]
- **hyaline state** — the translucent, unanchored, not-yet-committed condition a Mist is in (the Mist is the figure; the hyaline state the condition). [S47; ADR-U031]
- **accretion** — a Mist filling in as it answers the founding questions: eyes, mouth and features, definition, condensation; tracks progress, never trait. [S47; ADR-U031]
- **FIM** — FringeIsland Member: the base identity, a person with a Whisp, with their equal place in the Tree. "Member" is the platform synonym only. [roles core L0]
- **transcendence / metamorphosis** — one event, two names (platform / lore): the Mist condensing into the anchored glowing ball when all founding questions are answered AND consent is given; the first paying-out of the cord. [S47, S48; ADR-U031]
- **the delivery** — metamorphosis seen from the Whisp's side: the Whisp born into separateness across a kept cord (parturition, not genesis). [S48]
- **the cord** — the FIM–Whisp lifeline, FIM at the origin, Whisp at the far end; kept, not severed; its length is the FIM's dial of voluntariness and developmental intimacy; carries salience (maturity) and health (colour, integrity). [S27, S37, S40, S48]
- **the Void** — the axis of separation: wherever the Whisp is once it leaves the FIM, along the whole cord. [S27; cosmology §5]
- **the senses** — the five Big-5-mapped metaphorical perception channels that open as the FIM discloses to the Whisp; the world requires all five for full experience. [S18]
- **the map** — what hinders the FIM (their competing commitments and Big Assumptions); held by the Whisp, filled as Mara releases it. [S56, S57]
- **the transfer** — the Whisp's filling and Mara's emptying as one process seen from two ends. [S57]
- **the zones** — comfort → fear → learning → growth, with panic as the fence; the felt experience of the transfer. [S73, S74]
- **the panic fence** — the boundary the Whisp never opens, even on request: voluntariness sets the ceiling, care sets the floor. [S19, S74]
- **tough love** — the Whisp's stance: always wants its human well, never at the cost of avoiding what holds them back; disagrees with compassion. [S3]
- **internalised** — the endgame: the Whisp (and Mara) integral to the human, no longer separate, FringeIsland no longer needed; the FIM is whole. [S6, S7, S54]
- **the backdrop** — the discovered, never-told myth: the Whisp as the FIM's own future whole self reaching back; the future at stake; possible, not guaranteed. [S66–S72, S76]
- **the conviction** — the founders' public why in the MANIFESTO preamble; names no myth. [S76, S77]
- **NPC** — a world-inhabitant composed of up to three authorial layers (body — Creator; culture — Anthropologist; character — Teller), added in proportion to the depth its role demands; no Whisp. [S30]
- **Jake-and-Avatar structure** — the FIM safe body-side, the Whisp the projection that can be harmed and respawns; two views of one being. [S22]
- **meta-safety** — real felt stakes inside a frame that guarantees the human cannot be broken; harm reaches the Whisp, never the FIM. [S19, S22, S27]
- **DS-7 Intelligence / DS-1 World Model** — the two services that own the Whisp's being-face and world-presence face respectively; the AI is the medium through which the future self speaks. [ADR-U029, S69]

### Retired or superseded names

- **AI Mentor / Mentor** (for the AI companion) → **the Whisp**. [CQ-009 note; beings core]
- **Shadow** (as the anonymous entrant, S16/S39/S45/S46) → **Mist**; "Shadow" reassigned to Mara's form in Marath. [S47, S53; ADR-U031]
- **Visitor** (platform term) → **Mist**. [roles core; ADR-U004 → U027 → U031]
- **Guest** (in-session suggestion that a Shadow could visit the village as a guest) → wrong; the village is FIM-only. [S45]
- **"each FIM's personal future self"** (old README) → reconciled as **the FIM's own future, whole self reaching back**. [S67]
- **"clone" / "tool for the system to extract personality"** (S2 wording) → the two parallel completions; filled by the human growing. [S2 Held, S17; B-6]
- **"the Whisp is the FringeIsland mobile app's faculty" / the Gimbal as a device or app** (S10, S22, S26) → the Gimbal is the **senses-surface equipment profile**. [ADR-U025]
- **"affordance"** → **equipment**. [ADR-U025]
- **"amnesiac future self", "embodiment", "the future power", "the silent hero", "the Unintegrated Self", "growth mirror", "instrument (not mirror)"** (research and old README vocabulary) → not canon; the Whisp's origin is S67, its endgame "internalised" (S7), its counterpart Mara (S51).
- **Three Worlds / Safe Harbour / The Other Side; "the Beyond" for the far side** → Ordinary World → the Shimmer → two places, each with near side / far side / beyond. [cosmology core §What this supersedes; S52, S55]
- **Makers / Weavers / Skalds** → retired; the Dreamineer specialisations are Creator, Anthropologist, Teller, Wayfinder. [beings core; roles core]
- **"Agents, not scenery"** (S15 flat reading of NPCs) → layered, depth-on-demand composites; some NPCs are scenery-with-belonging by design. [S30]

## From growth
- **The three questions** — Who am I? What do I want? How do I get there?; held by the platform, answered by the member. [VISION Purpose; S6]
- **The red thread** — the three questions running through every journey, episode, group and conversation. [growth core]
- **Three Perspectives** — Individual (1), Relationship (1+1), Collective (1+Community): three simultaneous angles on the same human; synonyms in older text: Relational/Communal, self/relationship/self-in-community, "society". [S9; VISION]
- **9-cell matrix** — the three questions × the three perspectives; a structural description of the developmental space, not a curriculum. [growth core]
- **To collapse a perspective** — (undefined in the sources) to actually work a perspective's cells; the relational and communal perspectives cannot be collapsed alone. [growth core; VISION] — see B11.
- **Live / Grow / Matter** — the drives beneath the questions: engaged in the moment; curious, not deficient; mattering to oneself and through contribution to others; danced in balance, never in sequence. [S28; VISION]
- **Two-pole Matter** — the internal pole (I feel I matter; freedom) and the external pole (mattering to others / something bigger; contribution), bridged by influence. [S28]
- **Planned happenstance** — readiness to act on the unplanned; part of Grow. [S28]
- **Framing discipline** — "informed by / resonant with, never equals": plain language on top, science as substrate. [S28]
- **Growth is delight, not deficiency** — the guarded core value. [S28; VISION 6]
- **Built to graduate, not retain** — success is the member outgrowing the platform. [S6–S7; VISION 7]
- **Graduation / internalisation** — the Whisp becomes an integral part of the human; FringeIsland no longer needed. [S6–S7]
- **Whole** — the state in which the Whisp has filled and Mara has emptied, both internalised. [S54, S57]
- **The newborn arc** — unwritten and free → written by experience (Mara fills) → limits crack (Mara dissolves) → free again, knowingly. [S54]
- **Mara** — the negative inner voice, listened to unknowingly; full of content from the start; empties as the Whisp grows. [S51, S54]
- **The Shadow** — what Mara looks like when met in Marath; one per FIM; size grows with depth; met only along a shared branch, and then only in form and force. [S53, S59, S62]
- **The transfer** — the Whisp's filling and Mara's emptying as one process; growth as a transfer of authority from Mara to the Whisp. [S57]
- **Immunity to Change (ITC)** — Kegan & Lahey's account of why people who want to change do not: competing commitments and Big Assumptions as intelligent self-protection; the substrate beneath Mara. [S9, S56]
- **Big Assumption / competing commitment / Subject → Object** — ITC's terms: the hidden rule that runs you; the commitment that protects it; the move from being had by it to holding it. [S56; kegan §2]
- **Order of mind** — Kegan's qualitatively different ways of making meaning; ITC in the universe can scale up to a move between orders; the platform never labels a FIM's order. [S58]
- **Holding environment** — confirmation, contradiction, continuity: the substrate beneath the Whisp's tough love. [S74; kegan §1.3]
- **The zones** — comfort, fear, learning, growth; the felt experience of the transfer. [S73–S74]
- **The fence (panic zone)** — the zone the Whisp never opens; an overwhelming test strengthens the immunity. [S19, S74]
- **Voluntariness sets the ceiling, care sets the floor** — the FIM dials the depth of the invitation, never the outcome; the never-panic rule binds from the other side. [S58, S74]
- **Void distance** — how far the Whisp has ventured on the cord; the distance that carries growth pressure and risk; not bodily distance. [S27; cosmology §10]
- **The cord dial** — the FIM sets the cord's length: pay out to venture, reel in to retreat; also developmental intimacy. [S40, S48]
- **Breath-rhythm** — venture out for challenge, return home for safety. [S26; cosmology §10]
- **Meta-safety** — the world contains threat; the experience does not; the human is never the thing at risk. [S19, S22]
- **Respawn / rewind** — die inside the story, retry inside the story; the loop is the medium. [S19–S21; narrative Respawn]
- **Productive failure** — real felt stakes inside guaranteed reversibility. [S19]
- **Signature journey vs charter trip** — bespoke personalisation grown from disclosure breadth vs the same bus for everyone. [S19]
- **Assessment dissolved into dialogue** — validated instruments folded into the Whisp's curious questions, never questionnaires. [S17]
- **The senses** — the five Big 5 traits as the five senses of the parallel world; full experience needs all five. [S18]
- **Internal and external self-awareness** — who I am inside; how I appear and operate in the world. [S6]
- **Engagement spectrum** — the two ways of engaging as a FIM: cultivate your home, or go on expeditions near or far. [growth core (Stefan, 2026-09-16)]
- **Homebody** — tends the home, reflects quietly, works the individual perspective; a complete mode, not "behind". [growth core]
- **Explorer** — deep in arcs, episodes, quests, community; engages 1+1 and 1+community. [growth core]
- **"Beyond" (spectrum pole)** — the far end of the expedition way; NAME PENDING because it collides with the reach "beyond". [growth core; S52, S55]
- **Member archetypes (Elena, David, Astrid)** — provisional personas illustrating the spectrum; unvalidated. [growth core, Thinking grade]
- **Privacy tiers** — private by default; selectively shared; transparently shared. [privacy model]
- **Private by default** — the inner journey, home, journal, assessment data, cord health and Whisp filling; no opt-in needed. [privacy model]
- **Selectively shared** — per aspect, per audience, per region of the home; voluntary; revocable. [privacy model; S43]
- **Transparently shared** — opt-in groups with a norm of openness, by explicit informed consent of every participant. [privacy model]
- **The branch rule** — what is intimate (cord health, another's Shadow) is visible only along a grown branch: glanceable not diagnostic, invited not imposed, self first. [S37, S62]
- **Form and force, never content** — what a friend sees of your Shadow along a branch. [S62]
- **Stage legible, content private** — a Mist's accretion shows how far along, never which trait. [S47; ADR-U031]
- **Ephemerality** — a Mist's data and presence do not persist; metamorphosis is the persistence-and-consent threshold. [S46–S47; ADR-U031]
- **Rest (availability, not occupancy)** — the shelter is always there; the FIM is not meant to stay in it. [S78 image; Candidate C eighth pass — pending, see B12]
- **Research anchors** — dual continuum (Keyes), multi-system requirement, Three Perspectives meta-lens, Immunity to Change. [S8–S9]

Retired names and what replaced them:
- **Comfort → growth → panic (three zones)** → comfort → fear → learning → growth, panic as the fence. [S74]
- **"Safe Harbour" / "safe harbor" as the comfort zone or a world** → the village (far side of place 2) for the place; the comfort zone is a zone, not a place. [S26 → S41, S55; cosmology "What this supersedes"]
- **"The Beyond" (reach)** → the far side; the new further reach is "the beyond" (lowercase, unnamed). [S52, S55]
- **"Distance from FringeIsland" as the gradient** → Void distance on the cord. [S27]
- **"Shadow" as the anonymous entrant** → the Mist; "Shadow" now = Mara's form in Marath. [S47, S53]
- **"AI Mentor" / "Mentor"** → the Whisp. [CQ-009 note; beings core]
- **"Affordance"** → equipment. [ADR-U025]
- **"Assessment" / "test" as a questionnaire** → dissolved dialogue, the senses. [S17–S18]
- **"Avatar" as the FIM's representation (privacy-model title)** → the Whisp is the avatar; the FIM's representation is the ball / home. [S22, S43] — see B13.
- **"Void-dimensions" (three-dimensional Void model)** → the Void as an axis. [growth core footer; cosmology §5]
- **"Three Worlds" (Ordinary World / Safe Harbour / Other Side)** → Ordinary World → Shimmer → the Fringe (two places, three reaches). [cosmology "What this supersedes"; S52]
- **"Makers, Weavers, Skalds"** → Dreamineer specialisations (roles chapter; listed because the growth core's archetypes still say "Dreamineer" as a persona). [beings core]

## From story
- **season** — an authored unit of story, made of episodes, crafted by Tellers in Arc Studio [S13; roles core L1]. Cadence (four a year) is inherited platform design, not ruled [narrative core §Planned; B1].
- **episode** — the unit of story inside a season; the unit a whole-episode respawn replays [S13, S21].
- **journey** — a FIM's own path through the world, authored by Wayfinders in Journey Studio, alone / in a pair / in a group (= the Three Perspectives) [S13; roles core L1]. Nests seasons and episodes for respawn purposes: journey → season → episode → moment [S21 held].
- **arc** — the containing story that keeps holding a FIM through respawn ("the containing arc") [narrative core §Respawn]; also "a Teller's published Arc — a season or episode" [Candidate A #6, not locked].
- **A-plot / B-stories / sub-plots** — TV-craft terms in the narrative core's Overview, unratified [narrative core §Overview; B3].
- **signature journey vs charter trip** — bespoke to this FIM vs the same for everyone on the bus; disclosure breadth moves a journey along this continuum [S19].
- **route type** — Fixed / Hybrid / Traveler-Initiated / AI-Generative; a DS-3 registry, not universe canon [narrative core §Planned; B2]. Old name "Type 4 (AI-generative) journeys" [CQ-011].
- **content family** — Witness / Reflect / Decide / Act / Encounter / Rest; named in the planned journeys page only [narrative core §Planned; B2].
- **the Game** — a depth setting of journeys inside the one lived experience, not a product [ADR-U025 §5; VISION]. Retired: "the Game" as a third product entity (Unreal) [ADR-U025].
- **respawn** — the Whisp dying in a story and re-entering it; the FIM rewinds and retries [S19, S22]. **respawn topology** — the loop unit a story uses: event-local, round-bounded, day-bounded, episode-bounded [S20]. **loop texture** — the craft palette: combat practice, mystery-puzzle, constrained inquiry, reflective grief [S20].
- **the loop is the medium** — repetition is how the story works, not a safety net; what persists across the loop is part of what the story is about [S20; narrative core §Respawn].
- **home base** — a safe return point within the same journey/season/episode at which a FIM respawns [S21]; also the ball as "home base, seed-source, anchor-root" [S39] — one word, possibly two objects [B8].
- **episode-repeat** — respawn by replaying the whole episode [S21].
- **productive failure** — real felt stakes inside guaranteed reversibility; the condition under which deep learning happens [S19].
- **meta-safety** — the world contains threat; the experience of being in it does not; the FIM cannot be broken by the platform [S19].
- **felt stakes** — Jake does not want his Avatar hurt: caring about the projection gives the experience its weight without existential risk [S22].
- **the backdrop** — why the Whisp is here: the FIM's own future, whole self reaching back, because AI forks the future and growth into higher orders of mind is the lever [S66–S72]. Deep-bible tier; never told up front [S75–S76].
- **the dark branch** — the catastrophe future in the myth; the stake of the universe and the fundament of storytelling; never a whip [S66, S70, S73]. **the good future** — a world people want to belong to; a consequence the Whisp trusts in, never a target [S66, S68].
- **the conviction vs the myth** — the public real-world why (MANIFESTO) vs the discovered in-world story; available vs told [S76, S77].
- **deep bible / story bible / breadcrumbs / breadcrumb register** — proposed three knowledge tiers (full backdrop, held by canon keepers; rules and fixed points and what may be hinted, for Tellers and Wayfinders; hints in the world, for FIMs) plus a register of every hint and what it points to [S75, proposed; CQ-018; B9].
- **mythology arc vs free episode** — proposed: an arc that touches a fixed point or places a breadcrumb passes through the canon keepers; everything else is free, bound only by canon [S75; CQ-018 rec. 3].
- **canon keeper / showrunner** — proposed: a function inside Universe Studio that holds the deep bible; the founder is the first [S75; CQ-018 rec. 1].
- **fixed point** — proposed: a backdrop fact a mythology arc must not contradict (the Whisp's origin; the future at stake; what Marath's beyond is; the source of the Whisp's wisdom) [CQ-018 rec. 3].
- **the itch** — something that needs to be found interesting to gain curiosity; curiosity as a design principle [S70; S55; S76].
- **the zones** — comfort → fear → learning → growth, panic as the fence; the felt experience of the Mara-to-Whisp transfer [S73, S74]. Retired form: comfort → growth → panic [cosmology core §10; narrative core §Respawn].
- **the panic fence** — the boundary the Whisp never opens; a test too big to be safe strengthens the immunity [S74].
- **rest in the storm** — the butterfly image: rest is legitimate, protects what flies, and ends in flight; availability, not occupancy [S78, offered].
- **Teller** — the Dreamineer specialisation that authors seasons, episodes and named characters with arcs in Arc Studio; the particular, story scale [S29, S30; roles core]. **Wayfinder** — authors journeys in Journey Studio; the personal scale [S29, S30]. **Arc Studio / Journey Studio / Universe Studio** — the story and journey studios, and their parent and binding frame [S13, S14; ADR-U026]. Retired: "Arch Studio" (transcription drift) [S14]; Makers / Weavers / Skalds [roles core §Retired names]; "Weaver-AI collaboration tools" [CQ-011 old text].
- **NPC character layer** — the Teller-authored layer that makes an NPC a someone (name, history, wound, desire, arc); story-critical figures carry all three layers [S30; beings core].
- **the first hour / the first experience** — the founding narrative a newcomer first enters; the highest-risk gap; deferred until mechanics are firm [CQ-010; backlog]. Old name "Journey Zero" (the onboarding journey) [CQ-010].
- **Season Zero** — the Kickstarter as founding moment: backers arrive on the island, rewards are arrival rituals, campaign updates are the first episodes [community core]; not a season in the Arc Studio sense [B15].
- **immersive edutainment** — growth through being moved by stories that hold warmth and threat, life and death [S12; VISION].
- **the Hero's Journey** — the narrative framework named in VISION; not elaborated in discovery [VISION §What this is; B14].
- **"Alternative Reality"** — a logbook term for the reality-fiction blur; a design lineage (ARGs, analog horror), not canon [S23].
- **wound and cause** — every blight in the warm place has a cause in Marath; notice, cross, stop the cause, watch it heal [S32; cosmology core §3].
- **Retired names met in old story text:** "Shadow" for the anonymous entrant → the Mist (today's Shadow is Mara's form met in Marath) [S47, S53]; "Safe Harbour / The Other Side / Three Worlds" → the village / the Fringe (cosmology core "What this supersedes"); "the Beyond" → the far side, with a new unnamed beyond further out [S52, S55]; "affordance" → equipment [ADR-U025]; "FringeIsland" for place 2 → the universe's name, place 2's own name pending [S63–S65]; "AI Mentor" → the Whisp [project vocabulary; CQ-009 note].

## From roles-community
- **FIM** — FringeIsland Member: the base identity, a person with a Whisp, taking their equal place in the Tree. [roles L0; VISION]
- **Member** — the platform-technical synonym for FIM; used for nothing else. [roles L0]
- **Mist** — the anonymous entrant: a translucent, drifting becoming-figure that accretes form as it answers the founding questions; near-side only; ephemeral, unlinkable; has its own Whisp and cord from the start. [S47; ADR-U031]
- **Hyaline (state)** — the translucent, unanchored, not-yet-committed condition a Mist is in; the Mist is the figure, hyaline the condition. [S47; ADR-U031]
- **Accretion** — the Mist filling in (eyes → mouth → definition → condensation) as the founding questions are answered; tracks progress, never trait. [S47]
- **Transcendence** — the platform term for the one event that grants persistence, consent and the ball. [ADR-U031]
- **Metamorphosis** — the lore term for the same event: the Mist condensing into its glowing glass ball in the Tree; the cord's first paying-out. [S47; S48]
- **The ball** — the glowing glass ball granted at transcendence: home base, seed-source, anchor-root; a two-zone gateway (inside → home, rim → village). [S39; S42]
- **Dreamineer** — a FIM in their authorial, dream-it-into-being mode; permission-gated; four specialisations. From Disney's Imagineers. [S29]
- **Creator** — Dreamineer specialisation: the hard side of World Studio (physical substrate). [S29; S30]
- **Anthropologist** — Dreamineer specialisation: the soft side of World Studio (cultural substrate; the typical). [S29; S30]
- **Teller** — Dreamineer specialisation: Arc Studio (seasons, episodes, named characters; the particular). [S29; S30]
- **Wayfinder** — Dreamineer specialisation: Journey Studio (journeys alone / pairs / group; the personal). [S29; S30]
- **Steward** — per-group support role: leads and cares for a group. [roles L1]
- **Guide** — per-group support role: facilitates a joint journey. [roles L1]
- **Participant** — per-group support role: takes part (renamed from the group role "Member"). [roles L1]
- **Observer** — per-group support role: watches. [roles L1]
- **Universeers** — L2 seats caring for the constituency, the product portfolio, the community, economy, legal. [S29]
- **The FringeIsland Council** — L2 seat for major decisions and partner relations. [S29]
- **DeusEx** — the human root-admin group of the running platform; holds the authority of last resort; the link between platform life and ecosystem development. [ADR-U028]
- **The Console** — working name for the back-of-house surface of universe-scoped governance; fiction name deferred. [ADR-U028]
- **Governance by scope** — community care in place, universe governance on the Console; the same law tiers World Studio access. [ADR-U028; ADR-U026]
- **Universe Studio** — the parent and binding frame over World, Arc and Journey Studios. [S13–14; ADR-U026]
- **World Studio / Arc Studio / Journey Studio** — the three role-gated authoring modes (world, story, journeys). [ADR-U026]
- **Dreaming session** — a human-first facilitated ideation ritual before AI expands the field; AI facilitates, never generates. [S24; PRINCIPLES-AI]
- **Guard railing** — bidirectional, human-authored rails: constraining AI and protecting the human driver. [S24; PRINCIPLES-AI]
- **Equipment** — a device's capabilities (senses, canvas, precision input); the word "affordance" is retired. [ADR-U025]
- **Equipment profile** — a surface of the one experience defined by the equipment it needs, not by a device. [ADR-U025]
- **The Hub** — the canvas surface (refinement and depth, at the desk). [ADR-U025]
- **The Gimbal** — the senses surface (perception and capture, out in the world); in the fiction, the instrument that opens the Shimmer. [ADR-U025; S10; S52]
- **The Game** — not a product: a depth setting of journeys. [ADR-U025]
- **Referral** — a FIM-shared invite to the platform; membership follows transcendence-and-consent, never precedes it. [ADR-U040]
- **Season Zero** — the Kickstarter campaign as the founding moment and first chapter. [community core]
- **Founding Dreamineer cohort** — the world-builder-tier backers; the seed of the authorial community. [community core]
- **The FringeIsland Foundation** — the intended permanent holder of community ownership; not yet formally established. [MANIFESTO; CQ-006]
- **Three Perspectives** — Individual (1), Relationship (1+1), Collective (1+Community). [VISION]
- **Developmental interdependence** — growth is structurally social; the relational and collective perspectives need other people. [VISION principle 3]
- **Canon keeper (recommended)** — a function inside Universe Studio holding the deep bible; the founder first. [CQ-018]
- **Deep bible / story bible / breadcrumbs (recommended)** — the three knowledge tiers of the backdrop. [S75; CQ-018]

Retired names → replacement: Visitor → Mist; Shadow (entrant) → Mist (Shadow now = Mara's form in Marath); Member (group role) → Participant; Makers / Weavers / Skalds → the Dreamineer specialisations; FringeIsland Studio → World Studio; Arch Studio → Arc Studio; Three Worlds / Safe Harbour / The Other Side → Ordinary World / the village / the Fringe (two places); affordance → equipment; the Game (product) → journey depth; AI Mentor → the Whisp; the Member/Steward/Dreamineer/DeusEx ladder → the L0/L1/L2 model; "Dreamineer Council" → the FringeIsland Council; "the island" / "the FringeIsland place" for place 2 → place 2 (name pending; FringeIsland = the universe).
